# 第6級第四批｜6A-14《魔王遭全民罷免後，世界更危險了》

**The Demon King Was Voted Out**

**類型：** 政治奇幻、世界秩序  
**級距：** Level 6（5000–6000 字彙）  
**版本：** 拾頁 StorySteps｜第四批｜25 章完整教學初稿  
**正式配詞：** L6_A本_25章配詞.md，501 個原始 ID

## 故事簡介

魔王透過公開投票和平遭到罷免。當晚城市上空裂開，主持選務的伊莉絲發現，人人厭惡的統治者一直在維持世界完整。

**English premise:** The demon king is peacefully recalled by a public vote. That night the sky splits above the city, and Iris, the woman who managed the ballot, discovers that the ruler everyone hated was holding the world together.

## 世界規則

古老封印只能吸收自願守護者可測量的能量來減緩裂隙。封印能檢查、修理或分配各社區承擔，沒有統治者能從恐懼中憑空製造力量。

**English:** The ancient seals slowed the rift only by absorbing measurable energy from willing keepers. A seal could be inspected, repaired, or divided among communities; no ruler could manufacture that strength out of fear.

## 閱讀方法與編輯說明

每一個 EN 英文段落後緊接繁中對照段落；詞彙證據句須出現在同章英文正文中。部分高階配詞取自本專案既有教學語料，依新題材整理，**尚未完成逐句文學編輯及語意終校**。本書為完整章節的教學初稿，並非出版定稿。第10章設有核心反轉，第25章主線收束。中文詞義依情境，不代表配詞原檔官方翻譯。

---

## CHAPTER 01｜An Election Under a Red Sky｜紅天之下的選舉

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：648**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The demon king lost the election at sunset. At midnight, the moon cracked. Iris had spent six months making sure every ballot could be counted twice and every resident, human or demon, could challenge a false name. She had defended the recall against people who feared the king’s power. When the final numbers appeared, the crowd cheered and an orderly carried the empty throne into storage. King Orren said nothing. He removed his black ring, set it beside the royal warrant, and walked away without guards. Iris thought that the story had ended. Then every streetlamp in Blackgate went out at exactly the same moment.

**繁中** 魔王在日落時輸掉選舉。午夜時，月亮裂開了。伊莉絲花了六個月，確保每張選票都能重算兩次，也確保每位居民，不論人類或魔族，都可以對假姓名提出異議。她曾頂著畏懼魔王權力的反對者，捍衛這次罷免。最終票數公布時，群眾歡呼，勤務員把空王座搬進倉庫。魔王奧倫沒有說話。他取下黑色戒指，放在王室令狀旁，沒有帶守衛就離開。伊莉絲以為故事結束了，接著黑門城每盞路燈在同一瞬間熄滅。

**EN** The first crack appeared at the junction of the old market and the east wall. A cart wheel slipped into it; a porter with a limp pulled a frightened child clear just before the stones folded inward. It was not an earthquake. The movement followed the pale lines carved beneath the street. Iris had seen those lines in an archive, labeled an anchor system for ancient magic, but no report explained what they held in place. She ordered the gates closed and asked for a comparative survey of the damage. Hero Severin called the precautions an annoyance. “The world is finally free,” he said. “Stop looking for monsters.”

**繁中** 第一道裂縫出現在舊市集與東城牆交會處。馬車輪滑進裂口，一名跛腳搬運工趕在石塊向內折陷前把受驚的孩子拉出來。那不是地震，地面變形沿著街底刻出的蒼白線條延伸。伊莉絲曾在檔案裡看過這些線，標示為古代魔法的錨定系統，卻沒有報告解釋它們固定著什麼。她下令關閉城門，要求比較測量損傷。英雄賽維林稱這些預防措施只會添麻煩。「世界終於自由了，」他說，「別再到處找怪物。」

**EN** In the emergency hall, volunteers were broiling fish while families gathered around the last working furnace. A widow held a sleeping infant to her bosom; another woman looked through a donation crate for a warm shirt and bra after the collapse ruined her home. These ordinary needs made the disaster more real than any legend. A bank teller brought notice that a gangster had offered a bribe to make the maintenance fund disappear from public accounts. Iris refused to abbreviate the witness’s name, though the man trembled when the clerk read it aloud. The account was applicable to twelve districts, yet only one had ever been told why the money was collected.

**繁中** 緊急大廳裡，志工炙烤魚，家庭圍在最後一座仍能使用的暖爐旁。一名寡婦把睡著的嬰兒抱在胸前；另一位婦女因房子倒塌，在捐贈箱尋找暖和上衣和胸罩。這些日常需求比任何傳說都更能說明災難的真實。一名銀行職員帶來通知：黑幫分子曾提出賄賂，要讓維護基金從公共帳目消失。伊莉絲拒絕縮寫證人姓名，即使書記朗讀時那人全身發抖。這筆基金適用十二個街區，卻只有一區曾被告知收費原因。

**EN** Iris found an old engineer behind the furnace. One eyelid drooped from an injury he had received while repairing a seal twenty years earlier. He asked permission to narrate what the official history had omitted. The first demon king, he explained, had not conquered Blackgate. The city had elected him to carry a burden that no one community could bear alone. Every month he had moved some of that strain through an underground network. The power was finite; remove the black seals without replacing them, and the border between worlds would open. Iris looked at the new hero, who was already instructing workers to destroy the remaining seals.

**繁中** 伊莉絲在暖爐後方找到一名老工程師。他一隻眼皮因二十年前修補封印時受傷而下垂。他請求允許敘述官方歷史遺漏的部分。工程師說，第一位魔王不是征服黑門城，而是被城市選出來，承擔任何單一社區都無法獨力負荷的工作。每個月，他都透過地下網路分散負擔。力量是有限的；若拆掉黑色封印而沒有替代方案，兩個世界的邊界就會打開。伊莉絲看向新英雄，他已開始命令工人拆除剩下的封印。

**EN** “Do not summon the former king,” Severin warned. “His followers will only ask him to take the crown again.” Iris refused that false choice. Restoring a person’s power was not the same as consulting his knowledge. She wrote a temporary safety order on virgin parchment and invited representatives from every district to inspect the damaged lines. The city could flourish without a demon monarch, but only if the burden were visible and shareable. Her first proposal would require twenty-four volunteers and twelve hours of precise measurement. Severin demanded that she cancel it. Iris showed him the casualty reports and asked him to read them himself.

**繁中** 「別召回前魔王，」賽維林警告，「他的追隨者只會要求他重戴王冠。」伊莉絲拒絕這個假兩難。恢復一個人的權力，不等於向他請教知識。她在全新羊皮紙上寫下臨時安全令，邀請各區代表檢查受損線條。黑門城可以沒有魔王而繁榮，但前提是負擔必須看得見，也能共享。她的第一份方案需要二十四名志工和十二小時精密測量。賽維林要求取消，伊莉絲拿出傷亡報告，請他親自閱讀。

**EN** The old engineer raised the diagram toward the moon. Its crack had widened, and beyond the red edge something enormous seemed to turn in its sleep. Iris did not believe a problem became acceptable because a hero had inherited it. She sent a messenger after Orren, bearing neither an apology nor a crown, but one question: “What have you been keeping outside our sky?” When the messenger reached the empty road, a reply was already nailed to the gate. It was written in Orren’s hand: “Do not reopen the throne. Open the maintenance journal.” Beneath that sentence was a second warning. “You have six days before the first seal fails.”

**繁中** 老工程師把圖紙舉向月亮。裂縫已經變寬，紅色邊緣後方似乎有巨大之物在睡夢中轉身。伊莉絲不認為問題交給英雄繼承就會變得可以接受。她派信使追上奧倫，帶去的不是道歉或王冠，而是一個問題：「你一直把什麼擋在我們的天空之外？」信使抵達空路時，城門上已釘好回信。奧倫的筆跡寫著：「不要重開王座。打開維護日誌。」底下還有第二句警告：「第一枚封印將在六天後失效。」

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. Iris announces the legal recall of Demon King Orren while Hero Severin celebrates beneath a sky filled with red clouds.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 伊莉絲宣布魔王奧倫依法遭到罷免，英雄賽維林在血紅雲層下慶祝。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. When Orren removes his signet, a crack appears across the moon and the city’s clocks stop.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 奧倫摘下印戒時，月亮出現裂縫，城市所有鐘錶同時停擺。


## CHAPTER 02｜The Throne Room Without a King｜沒有魔王的王座廳

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：854**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Severin occupies the palace and orders the black seals removed as symbols of tyranny. Iris asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。賽維林接管宮殿，命人拆掉象徵暴政的黑色封印。伊莉絲請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** The ancient seals slowed the rift only by absorbing measurable energy from willing keepers. A seal could be inspected, repaired, or divided among communities; no ruler could manufacture that strength out of fear.

**繁中** 古老封印只能吸收自願守護者可測量的能量來減緩裂隙。封印能檢查、修理或分配各社區承擔，沒有統治者能從恐懼中憑空製造力量。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Iris asked Orren not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。伊莉絲請奧倫不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The imperial maps described the people of the upper valley as aboriginal clans; the term was not an excuse to erase their treaties. Small settlements abound along the ridges, where families tended terraced gardens under extreme weather. Iris tried to align their route with the royal charts, but the marked crossing had been destroyed months earlier. The substation's altitude made it safe from river water but difficult for older residents to reach. The magistrate concealed his annoyance when Iva asked him to repeat a legal question slowly. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 帝國地圖把上游居民稱為原住部族，但這種稱呼並不能成為抹去他們條約的理由。 山脊遍布小聚落，家家在嚴酷天候下照顧梯田。 伊莉絲試著讓路線與王室地圖對齊，卻發現標示的渡口早在數月前就毀了。 變電站的海拔讓它不易淹水，卻使年長居民難以前往。 伊娃請地方法官慢慢重述法律問題時，他掩飾住自己的不耐。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. A nursery wall collapses where one seal stood, although no enemy has attacked. Iris watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Orren recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。一面曾立有封印的育兒院牆突然坍塌，現場卻沒有任何敵人入侵。伊莉絲看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。奧倫公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Iva gave a blunt answer: her neighbors had agreed to store grain, never to lose their homes. The clerk entered crossing in the testimony without deciding what it proved. Maelin would not discard a damaged draft until both sides had inspected its surviving lines. The river guard received a medical discharge note but still had to answer questions about his shift. Their joint endeavor would be assessed through measurements, not a flattering press release. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 伊娃直截了當地回答：鄰居同意保管糧食，從未同意失去家園。 書記將「crossing」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 伊莉絲不願在雙方檢查殘存字句前丟棄破損草稿。 河岸衛兵拿到出院證明後，仍須回答有關值班的問題。 雙方共同努力將以測量結果評估，而非以討好的新聞稿。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Iris insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Orren drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。伊莉絲堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。奧倫畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The first hurdle was proving that the old seal belonged to an actual village council. Lira changed her posture when the resident began speaking, listening without interruption. The interpreter demanded precision in the word for custody because a careless synonym could transfer ownership. The officer announced that travelers must undergo rigorous questioning, and that no royal family member could veto the security rules. At a wind-battered terrace, the party found an elderly widow repairing her roof. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 第一道障礙是證明舊印章確實屬於村議會。 居民開始發言時，莉拉調整姿勢，沒有打斷對方。 譯員要求精確使用「保管」一詞，因為草率的同義詞可能轉移所有權。 軍官宣布旅人都須接受嚴格盤查，任何王室成員也不能否決安全規則。 在飽受狂風吹襲的梯田邊，隊伍找到一名修屋頂的年長寡婦。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A workers' uprising was not the issue; they were requesting the safety rights already promised. Someone had ordered soldiers to usher displaced families down the mountain, while sending the food intended for them to private storehouses. The versatile backup unit could power either a clinic refrigerator or an emergency radio. The local council chose not to veto the temporary food route merely because the palace disliked it. The widow refused payment but asked that her testimony be carried to an independent court. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 這不是工人起義；他們只是要求已被承諾的安全權利。 有人下令士兵引導流離失所的家庭下山，卻把原本供應他們的食物送到私人倉庫。 多功能備用裝置可供應診所冰箱或緊急無線電。 地方議會沒有只因王宮不喜歡就否決臨時糧食路線。 寡婦拒絕收錢，只求她把證詞交給獨立法庭。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Iris passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Orren paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，伊莉絲經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。奧倫停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Severin occupies the palace and orders the black seals removed as symbols of tyranny.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 賽維林接管宮殿，命人拆掉象徵暴政的黑色封印。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. A nursery wall collapses where one seal stood, although no enemy has attacked.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 一面曾立有封印的育兒院牆突然坍塌，現場卻沒有任何敵人入侵。


## CHAPTER 03｜The Maintenance Journal｜維護日誌

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：861**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. Iris discovers a journal detailing monthly repairs to invisible boundaries beneath the streets. Iris checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。伊莉絲發現一本日誌，詳記城市街道下方看不見的邊界每月所需維修。伊莉絲核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** The ancient seals slowed the rift only by absorbing measurable energy from willing keepers. A seal could be inspected, repaired, or divided among communities; no ruler could manufacture that strength out of fear.

**繁中** 古老封印只能吸收自願守護者可測量的能量來減緩裂隙。封印能檢查、修理或分配各社區承擔，沒有統治者能從恐懼中憑空製造力量。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Iris listened longer than the schedule allowed. Orren reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。伊莉絲聽得比議程允許的更久。奧倫提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An advisory plaque still praised the Empire's conquest of the eastern frontier, but most houses were empty. The hill envoy rejected the word conquest because the agreement described cooperation rather than military victory. Vask called the missing binding a conspiracy invented by people who wanted to embarrass the crown. Someone had left a cucumber sandwich beside the abandoned recording equipment. The portable radio emitted a feeble signal from the edge of the substation. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 告示牌仍讚揚帝國征服東部邊境，但大多數房屋已空無一人。 山地使者反對使用「征服」，因為協議寫的是合作而非軍事勝利。 瓦斯克把遺失裝訂線稱為企圖羞辱王室的人編造的陰謀。 有人在廢置錄音設備旁留下一份小黃瓜三明治。 便攜無線電從變電站邊緣發出微弱訊號。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. The handwriting matches Orren’s, but he never asked the citizens to know what he had done. Iris read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Orren did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。筆跡屬於奧倫，他卻從未讓市民知道自己做過什麼。伊莉絲把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。奧倫沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A firecracker exploded outside the gate, and the officers checked whether it was a signal or prank. The mechanic began to fume when his accurate warning was described as a rumor. The delivery map was stained with grease from the night-shift kitchen. A careful inspection might lessen the danger to villages still connected to the hidden pipes. An abandoned river liner stood at the dock, its passenger deck still painted with cheerful blue stars. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 門外一枚爆竹炸響，官員檢查究竟是暗號還是惡作劇。 技師見自己的準確警告被說成謠言，開始氣憤。 送貨地圖沾有夜班廚房的油脂。 仔細檢查或許能減輕仍連著暗管的村莊所面臨的危險。 一艘廢棄的河上客輪停在碼頭，乘客甲板上仍畫著開朗的藍色星星。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Hero Severin demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Iris replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當英雄賽維林要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。伊莉絲回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: In the square stood a fire-blackened orphanage that the royal papers called a rebel fortress. Residents compared the recurring blackouts to a plague that never seemed to end. The town's clinic had been converted into a recreational hall for occupying soldiers. The video editor warned that online debate had become savage toward the unknown driver. A broken swing moved in the wind beside a shrub growing through the steps. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 廣場上有一間被火燒黑的孤兒院，王室報紙卻稱它是叛軍要塞。 居民把反覆神殿沉默比作似乎永不結束的災難。 鎮上的診所被占領軍改為休閒廳。 影像編輯警告網路討論對不明司機已變得殘酷。 破損的鞦韆在風中搖動，階梯縫裡長出灌木。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The technician explained that a local slang term had been mistaken for an equipment code. The second camera could surpass the first in clarity without identifying the people shown. For workers the damaged seal came to symbolize years of ignored safety warnings. A textile pattern on the borrowed coat helped establish when it had been photographed. The clinic kept its vaccine refrigerator running while the citywide evidence review continued. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 技師解釋某個地方俚語被誤認成設備代碼。 第二台攝影機雖然清晰度勝過第一台，仍無法辨認人物。 對工人而言，破損封條象徵多年被忽略的安全警告。 外借大衣的紡織圖案協助確認它何時被拍攝。 全市證據審查期間，診所仍維持疫苗冰箱運作。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Iris resisted the urge to call it proof. Orren opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。伊莉絲克制住把它當成證明的衝動。奧倫翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. Iris discovers a journal detailing monthly repairs to invisible boundaries beneath the streets.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 伊莉絲發現一本日誌，詳記城市街道下方看不見的邊界每月所需維修。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The handwriting matches Orren’s, but he never asked the citizens to know what he had done.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 筆跡屬於奧倫，他卻從未讓市民知道自己做過什麼。


## CHAPTER 04｜The Girl at the Rift｜裂隙旁的女孩

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：844**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. A child living near the eastern gate hears voices calling from the crack in the sky. Iris knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。東門附近的女孩聽見天空裂縫裡有人呼喚。伊莉絲知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** Orren arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Iris could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 奧倫帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。伊莉絲感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The cleaning team used bleach only after investigators recorded the stains near the door. The union requested a comprehensive list of every visitor with access to the storage area. A credible witness described what she heard but refused to guess who issued the command. Lira praised the workers' dedication to preserving damaged equipment instead of concealing it. The guest room held an old dresser and a thermometer with a cracked glass stem. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 調查員記錄門旁污漬後，清潔隊才使用漂白水。 工會要求完整列出所有能進入儲藏區的訪客。 可信的證人描述聽到的話，拒絕猜測誰下命令。 莉拉讚賞工人保存損壞設備而非藏匿的投入。 客房裡有一座舊五斗櫃，以及玻璃管裂開的溫度計。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. The voices repeat the names of people whose lives were saved by the demon king’s unpopular levies. For a long moment no one spoke. Iris understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Orren broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。那些聲音反覆念出曾被魔王不受歡迎的徵稅救下的人名。一時之間無人開口。伊莉絲明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。奧倫打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The guard's familiarity with the corridor helped reconstruct the route taken that night. The palace hierarchy placed interpreters below ministers, although their errors could affect entire villages. The landlady of the nearby boarding house remembered an unusual delivery before dawn. Beyond it, the marsh looked lush and peaceful, concealing collapsed drainage channels. The family asked to mourn their lost daughter without displaying her private diary. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 警衛對走廊的熟悉協助重建當晚路徑。 王宮階層把譯員置於大臣之下，他們的錯誤卻可能影響整個村莊。 附近寄宿屋的女房東記得黎明前一件異常送貨。 更遠處的沼澤看似草木茂盛而平靜，實際卻藏著崩毀的排水道。 家屬希望能悼念亡女，而不必公開展示她的私人日記。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Iris had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Orren proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 伊莉絲原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。奧倫提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The teacher hoped a fair treaty would nurture trust among children speaking different languages. Rain pressed against the windows, and the river threatened to overflow its banks. A cleaner had begun to overhear the order before the supervisor closed the door. The company's new report could refute a rumor only by presenting verifiable records. A gull circled the quay where a long-abandoned concert hall once hosted a symphony every spring. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 老師希望公平條約能培養使用不同語言的孩子間的信任。 雨水敲打窗戶，河水幾乎溢出河岸。 主管關門前，清潔員已無意間聽到那項命令。 公司新報告只能用可驗證紀錄反駁謠言。 一隻海鷗盤旋在碼頭上，附近那座廢棄音樂廳從前每年春天都會演奏交響樂。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The site superintendent admitted that he had approved a broken gate's temporary use. A recorded symphony played in the lobby, strangely calm beside the anxious witnesses. The clerk entered thermometer in the testimony without deciding what it proved. Lira said transparent testimony mattered more than turning the hearing into public turmoil. A little girl brought them a bowl of wholesome bean soup, then asked whether soldiers would take her brother next. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 現場總監承認自己曾批准暫時使用損壞閘門。 大廳播放錄製的交響樂，與焦慮證人形成奇特反差。 書記將「thermometer」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 莉拉說透明證詞比把聽證變成公眾騷動更重要。 一個小女孩送來一碗營養的豆湯，接著問士兵是否也會帶走她的哥哥。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Iris returned to a broken black seal. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Orren remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the city of Blackgate waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，伊莉絲再次看向一枚裂開的黑色封印。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。奧倫守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的黑門城等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. A child living near the eastern gate hears voices calling from the crack in the sky.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 東門附近的女孩聽見天空裂縫裡有人呼喚。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The voices repeat the names of people whose lives were saved by the demon king’s unpopular levies.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 那些聲音反覆念出曾被魔王不受歡迎的徵稅救下的人名。


## CHAPTER 05｜A Hero's First Order｜英雄的第一道命令

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：836**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. Severin cancels the maintenance levy and offers a feast with the recovered funds. Iris had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the city of Blackgate, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。賽維林取消維護稅，並用收回的款項舉辦盛宴。伊莉絲工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在黑門城，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Iris asked Orren to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。伊莉絲請奧倫說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Several students quietly began to rejoice; others feared that the academy would be closed for allowing dissent. A false accusation against the warehouse team was withdrawn after the access records were checked. A banquet was taking place across the courtyard, where donors praised the war effort. A relief grant originally intended for fertilizer purchases had been redirected without explanation. The riverside fishery requested a dedicated backup line to protect its refrigerated catch. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 幾名學生悄悄歡欣，其他人則害怕學院因容許異議而被關閉。 查核門禁紀錄後，對倉庫團隊的不實指控遭撤回。 庭院對面正在舉行宴會，捐助者讚美戰爭。 原訂購買肥料的救助補助遭不明轉用。 河岸漁業要求專用備電線路，以保護冷藏漁獲。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. By morning the wells begin pouring cold ash instead of water. Iris felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。隔天清晨，水井不再出水，反而冒出冰冷的灰燼。伊莉絲一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The theft allegation began to fracture under the weight of its own chronology. Technicians learned to harness surplus power without bypassing the required safety checks. Each operator wore headphones to hear control-room instructions over the storm warnings. The clinic reviewed its oxygen intake records to calculate emergency battery demand. The label preserved a young sculptor's maiden name instead of replacing it with her husband's. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 竊取指控開始因自身時間線的矛盾而瓦解。 技師學習利用剩餘電力，不繞過必要安全檢查。 每位操作員戴耳機，以在風暴警報聲中聽清控制室指示。 診所檢視氧氣攝入紀錄，計算緊急電池需求。 標籤保留年輕雕刻家的婚前姓氏，未以丈夫姓氏取代。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Hero Severin and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Iris rejected both. The issue was who carries the burden of keeping the world intact, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住英雄賽維林的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。伊莉絲兩者都拒絕。核心是誰有責任承擔維持世界完整的代價，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A young apprentice described an accusation against Daven: he had supposedly stolen a design for offshore heat collectors from the academy. Now she allowed them to enter a sealed lecture hall, but only as prospective witnesses, not celebrated heroes. A resident asked for a referendum on the long-term public ownership of the canal land. Daven invited Lira into the old workshop where they had learned to refine copper anchors. The nurses could finally rejoice when the new clinic batteries passed their first test. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一名年輕學徒說，達文據稱偷走學院的離岸熱能採集器設計。 如今她允許他們進入封閉講堂，但只能以潛在證人身分入內，不能當成受歡迎的英雄。 一名居民要求針對運河土地的長期公共所有權舉辦公投。 達文帶莉拉進入舊工坊，他們以前在這裡學習精煉銅製媒介。 新診所電池通過首次測試後，護理師終於能高興起來。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Some shareholders began to resent the safety veto, calling it an obstacle to profit. The operating room remained serene after its generator switched smoothly to backup power. A neighborhood pastor gave a short sermon about caring for people before property. The board refused to shuffle expenses between accounts to make the trial look cheaper. A tenant farmer testified that the governor had collected payment on land he did not own. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 部分股東開始怨恨安全否決權，稱它阻礙獲利。 手術室的發電機順利切換備電後，室內依舊平靜。 社區牧師簡短講道，提醒人們應先照顧人，再談財產。 董事會拒絕在帳戶間調換支出，營造試辦更便宜的假象。 一名佃農作證說，總督在不屬於他的土地上收取費用。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Iris had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Orren wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Hero Severin. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。伊莉絲知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。奧倫把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起英雄賽維林注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.

   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 賽維林取消維護稅，並用收回的款項舉辦盛宴。

## CHAPTER 06｜The Banished Engineer｜被流放的工程師

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：865**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Iris finds an engineer exiled for warning that the black seals were a form of public infrastructure. Iris saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。伊莉絲找到遭放逐的工程師，他曾警告眾人黑色封印其實是公共基礎設施。伊莉絲明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Iris could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Orren suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，伊莉絲無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。奧倫提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The city council could allege misconduct, but Maelin asked it to identify the specific page and signature. People had tried to beautify the hall with flowers for the midsummer festival, but protest notices covered the gates. Maelin traced the caption to a committee meeting where no qualified river interpreter had been present. The contract dispute reached its climax when the two shipping weights appeared together. Nessa used a special lamp to extract faint writing from beneath the later ink without damaging the sheet. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 市議會可以指控不當行為，但伊莉絲要求指出具體頁碼與簽名。 人們原本用花朵美化大廳，迎接仲夏節，如今大門卻貼滿抗議公告。 伊莉絲查出圖說來自一場沒有合格河語口譯員出席的委員會議。 當兩張出貨重量表一起出現時，契約爭議達到高潮。 妮莎利用特殊燈具辨認後加墨水下的淡字，沒有損壞紙張。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. His blueprints show that Orren's personal power was only one component; the council could distribute the load. Orren asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Iris agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。他的藍圖證明奧倫的力量只是一部分，議會原本可以分擔負荷。奧倫詢問是否可能只是出錯，伊莉絲也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A delegation of academy students had arrived after an extracurricular debate class. Their teacher was hospitable, offering water and a shady bench to witnesses who had traveled far. The usually jolly driver grew quiet when he recognized the private auction seal. Workers offered to mow the neglected path so elderly witnesses could reach the hall. The clerk, a cheerful man with a large mustache, agreed to write the words exactly as spoken. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 一群學院學生參加課外辯論課後來到法庭。 他們的老師十分好客，為遠道而來的證人準備飲水與陰涼長椅。 平日開朗的車夫認出私人拍賣印章時沉默了。 工人提議修剪荒廢小徑，方便年長證人到會堂。 留著大鬍子的開朗書記答應逐字記錄。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Iris insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Orren drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。伊莉絲堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。奧倫畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A grandmother told him to stop nagging and gave him an ounce of sweet tea. The old cafeteria became an oasis for night workers exhausted by the investigation. The sealing wax weighed less than an ounce, yet its broken edge offered a useful comparison. The civic court met beside a public garden on the outskirts of the capital. In the afternoon, a member of parliament appeared with a packet of engineering drawings. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一位祖母要他別再嘮叨，遞給他一小杯甜茶。 舊餐廳成為調查期間疲憊夜班工人的休息綠洲。 封蠟不到一盎司重，破損邊緣卻提供有用的比對依據。 公民法庭位於京城郊區的公共花園旁。 下午，一名國會議員帶著工程圖出席。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The nobleman's methods were ruthless but often lawful on paper: he delayed funding, demanded impossible permits, and threatened public employees with dismissal. A sculptor among them carried a model of the underground chambers, built from plaster so ordinary citizens could understand the design. The repair team preferred simplicity in emergency instructions over impressive technical language. A simultaneous release of both chambers was therefore forbidden until the second inspection. Public confidence fell into a slump when the palace refused to explain the disputed caption. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 這名貴族的方法冷酷，但在紙面上常常合法：延後經費、索取不可能取得的許可，並以解雇威脅公務員。 其中一位雕塑家帶著石膏製的地下艙室模型，好讓一般市民看懂設計。 維修團隊偏好簡單的緊急指示，而非炫目的技術詞。 因此，在第二次檢查以前，兩個艙室不得同時放能。 王宮拒絕解釋爭議圖說，公眾信心因此陷入低潮。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Iris passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Orren paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，伊莉絲經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。奧倫停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. Iris finds an engineer exiled for warning that the black seals were a form of public infrastructure.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 伊莉絲找到遭放逐的工程師，他曾警告眾人黑色封印其實是公共基礎設施。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. His blueprints show that Orren's personal power was only one component; the council could distribute the load.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 他的藍圖證明奧倫的力量只是一部分，議會原本可以分擔負荷。


## CHAPTER 07｜Terms of a Truce｜停戰條件

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：855**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Iris refused to begin with the official conclusion. Iris approaches Orren in exile and asks him to teach the new government how to repair the rift. In the city of Blackgate, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。伊莉絲拒絕從官方結論開始。伊莉絲在流放地找到奧倫，請他教新政府修補裂隙。在黑門城，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Iris asked Orren not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。伊莉絲請奧倫不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Through its windows she could see a garden of one hundred acre plots, each carefully divided among noble families. A wealthy bachelor on the board objected to being excluded from the safety committee. Employees organized a charity boxing exhibition to support families hurt by the outage. A disciplinary notice punished the worker for refusing to move an unsealed crate. An emigrant returning home discovered that the revised map no longer showed her family's grazing route. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 窗外花園分成大片土地，每塊以一英畝計算，整齊分給貴族家族。 董事會上一位富有的單身漢反對自己被排除在安全委員會外。 員工舉辦慈善拳擊表演，支援圍城受影響的家庭。 一份紀律處分通知懲罰工人拒搬未封箱子。 一名返鄉移民發現新版地圖不再顯示家族放牧路線。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Orren agrees only if citizens can inspect every cost he once concealed. Iris watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Orren recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。奧倫同意，但前提是人民能查核所有他曾隱瞞的代價。伊莉絲看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。奧倫公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Maelin refused to equate a temporary lease with permanent ownership simply because one English verb sounded convenient. The manager's confident account began to falter when Iris produced the dated gate book. Farmers argued that the fertility of the floodplain depended on seasonal access to both banks. Halda refused to forsake the elderly workers merely to recover the Tide Star sooner. A gay couple from the quarry asked that both partners be recognized in the housing register. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 伊莉絲拒絕只因一個英語動詞方便，就把暫時租賃等同永久所有權。 瑪拉拿出附日期的大門簿後，經理自信的說法開始動搖。 農民指出，沖積平原的土壤肥力仰賴兩岸季節性通行。 哈爾妲拒絕為了早點找回星盤而拋棄年長工人。 採石場的一對男同志伴侶要求住房登記承認雙方身分。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Hero Severin demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Iris replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當英雄賽維林要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。伊莉絲回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The workplace gloom lifted slightly after the tribunal protected the workers' safety veto. A survey found the dangerous intersection near the substation lacked proper lighting. A landslide had closed the direct quarry road, giving the driver one legitimate reason to detour. The local school proposed a food program to nourish children while grain ownership remained unresolved. The orthodox court scholars disliked the river dialect but admitted its grammar was legally relevant. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 法庭保護工人安全否決權後，工作場所的陰鬱稍稍散去。 調查發現變電站旁危險路口缺少適當照明。 山崩曾封閉採石場直路，讓司機有合理繞道原因。 當糧食所有權仍未解決時，地方學校提出供餐計畫滋養孩童。 正統宮廷學者不喜歡河語方言，卻承認其文法具有法律相關性。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The prohibition on unsafe work could not be canceled through a private annex. Lira asked for the provisional orders issued before the border fire; Lira said they had been destroyed in a flood. The vote was unanimous, yet the council could not erase an independent court order. The governor's vanity had led him to display a larger map than anyone could read closely. A woodpecker tapped the hostel roof while the workers discussed their missing wages. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 禁止危險作業的規定不能透過私人附約取消。 莉拉要求查看邊境大火前發布的臨時命令，莉拉卻說文件在洪水中毀了。 表決結果一致，議會卻無法抹除獨立法庭的命令。 總督的虛榮使他展示了一張大得無法近看細讀的地圖。 工人討論積欠薪資時，一隻啄木鳥敲著宿舍屋頂。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Iris resisted the urge to call it proof. Orren opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。伊莉絲克制住把它當成證明的衝動。奧倫翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

| L6-0995 | `woodpecker` | n. | `woodpecker` | 啄木鳥 | A woodpecker tapped the hostel roof while the workers discussed their missing wages. |

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. Iris approaches Orren in exile and asks him to teach the new government how to repair the rift.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 伊莉絲在流放地找到奧倫，請他教新政府修補裂隙。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Orren agrees only if citizens can inspect every cost he once concealed.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 奧倫同意，但前提是人民能查核所有他曾隱瞞的代價。


## CHAPTER 08｜A Town That Refused Fear｜拒絕恐懼的城鎮

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：851**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. A border town keeps its own maintenance seal intact and survives a night of violent tremors. Iris asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。邊境小鎮保住自己的維護封印，撐過強烈震動的一夜。伊莉絲請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Iris listened longer than the schedule allowed. Orren reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。伊莉絲聽得比議程允許的更久。奧倫提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An accumulation of sediment had changed the waterways, and the official map was no longer accurate. A blond clerk named Ruen carried a beverage to a retired teacher who would testify about missing children. The blond driver in the delivery photograph confirmed that he had used the wrong route. Lieutenant Lira sought clearance for the witnesses, while Daven asked a local surveyor to certify the paths through the marsh. The contractor lacked the necessary clearance to enter the northern storehouse after closing. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 累積的泥沙改變了水道，官方地圖已不準確。 一名金髮書記魯恩端飲料給退休教師，她將為失蹤的孩子作證。 送貨照片裡的金髮司機證實自己走錯了路。 莉拉中尉替證人申請通行許可，達文則請地方測量員認證穿越濕地的路線。 承包商沒有閉館後進入北倉所需的通行許可。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. The town's mayor admits having criticized Orren while secretly following his instructions. Iris read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Orren did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。鎮長承認自己一邊批評奧倫，一邊私下照做維護指示。伊莉絲把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。奧倫沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A copy of those figures survived in the harbor office, where a careful official had refused to deduct unexplained losses. The fragile astrolabe was not destined for a private cabinet merely because its label said so. The survey team had to exert care when lifting the old storage chest from damp floorboards. The porter rejected the heroic story that he had single-handedly saved the entire collection. A retired army lieutenant volunteered to organize transport without taking control of the inquiry. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 港務處保留了一份數字副本，一名謹慎的官員拒絕扣除來源不明的損失。 脆弱星盤不會只因標籤這樣寫，就注定進入私人收藏櫃。 測量團隊從潮濕地板抬起舊儲藏箱時必須格外用力且小心。 搬運員拒絕那個把他說成單槍匹馬救出全部收藏的英雄故事。 一名退役陸軍中尉志願安排運輸，沒有接管調查。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Iris had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Orren proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 伊莉絲原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。奧倫提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Literate workers could read the manual, but the team also prepared diagrams for everyone else. The provincial court stood beside a swamp where lotus flowers opened at dawn. That error had made their petitions appear marginal, even when hundreds signed them. The seals showed an overlap of ink from two different periods, prompting further laboratory tests. A provincial museum offered to store the disputed frame while the title hearing proceeded. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 識字的工人能閱讀手冊，團隊也替其他人準備圖解。 省級法庭位於沼澤旁，黎明時蓮花盛開。 這個錯誤使他們的請願看來無足輕重，即使有數百人簽名也一樣。 印章顯示兩個年代的墨跡重疊，因此需要進一步化驗。 一所地方博物館願在所有權聽證期間保管爭議框架。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The examiner disclosed her qualification in metal conservation and her previous work for the crown. Outside, the retired teacher offered the group simple refreshments. The team tried to retrieve a broken seal without disturbing the surrounding evidence. The original owner had learned navigation in the swamp beyond the museum's mapped territory. The children learned that a translator's vocation involved responsibility to readers, not blind loyalty to rulers. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 鑑定人公開自己的金屬修復資格及曾替王室工作的經歷。 法庭外，退休教師為一行人準備簡單茶點。 團隊試圖取回破損封條，同時不干擾周圍證物。 原所有者曾在博物館地圖之外的沼澤學習導航。 孩子們學到翻譯家的職志是對讀者負責，而非盲目效忠統治者。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Iris returned to a broken black seal. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Orren remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the city of Blackgate waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，伊莉絲再次看向一枚裂開的黑色封印。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。奧倫守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的黑門城等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. A border town keeps its own maintenance seal intact and survives a night of violent tremors.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 邊境小鎮保住自己的維護封印，撐過強烈震動的一夜。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. The town's mayor admits having criticized Orren while secretly following his instructions.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 鎮長承認自己一邊批評奧倫，一邊私下照做維護指示。


## CHAPTER 09｜The Price of a Seal｜一枚封印的價格

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：837**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. Iris tests a temporary seal with volunteers who can stop the work whenever the strain grows dangerous. Iris checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。伊莉絲與志工測試臨時封印，所有人都能在負荷過高時停手。伊莉絲核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** Orren arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Iris could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 奧倫帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。伊莉絲感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The public statement might alienate frightened investors, but hiding the altered seal would be worse. A black blot on the purchase order concealed the first digit of a serial number. Residents shared chili soup while waiting for the procurement hearing to begin. The envoy explained that continuity of village access was guaranteed in the oldest agreement. The cumulative delays had increased costs far beyond the original repair estimate. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 公開聲明可能疏遠害怕的投資人，但隱瞞改動封條更糟。 採購單上的黑墨漬蓋住序號第一個數字。 居民等候採購聽證開始時，分享辣椒湯。 使者解釋，最早的協議保障村民持續使用這條路；後來的修正案逐漸刪除渡口相關文字，累積的省略使權利在沒有任何正式廢止令的情況下消失。 累積延誤讓成本遠超原始維修估算。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. The results prove that no single person must be forced to suffer indefinitely. For a long moment no one spoke. Iris understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Orren broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。結果證明，沒有任何一個人必須被迫無限期承受痛苦。一時之間無人開口。伊莉絲明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。奧倫打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Lira made a decisive choice to publish the damaged-cabinet test before the vote. No worker was dispensable merely because an executive feared an embarrassing question. A visitor wore jasmine perfume that matched the scent noted by the night guard. The river lodge provided temporary rooms for families displaced during repairs. A maple tree near the entrance helped the courier identify the delivery location. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 莉拉果斷決定在表決前公布受損櫃體測試。 不能因主管害怕尷尬提問，就把任何工人當成可有可無。 某位訪客使用茉莉香水，與夜班警衛記下的氣味一致。 河邊旅舍為維修期間搬離的家庭提供臨時房間。 入口旁的楓樹協助快遞員辨認送貨地點。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Hero Severin and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Iris rejected both. The issue was who carries the burden of keeping the world intact, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住英雄賽維林的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。伊莉絲兩者都拒絕。核心是誰有責任承擔維持世界完整的代價，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The mistress of the house showed a dated guest book recording the translator's last visit. In the next room, people debated how to mobilize villagers for a public meeting without provoking soldiers. Maelin began to muse about how many borders had been changed by ordinary-looking words. The whistleblower's persistence finally persuaded investigators to examine the old account. A socialist council member supported public oversight without claiming every private firm was guilty. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 女主人出示附日期的訪客簿，記錄老譯員最後一次來訪。 隔壁房裡，人們討論如何動員村民出席公開集會，又不激怒士兵。 伊莉絲開始沉思，究竟有多少邊界是被看似普通的字詞改變。 吹哨者的堅持終於讓調查員檢查舊帳戶。 一名社會主義議員支持公共監督，沒有宣稱所有私人企業都有罪。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Employees had a chance to socialize after the hearing without being questioned by reporters. The access log covered a span of seven hours, with one unexplained gap near midnight. The author had been ordered to trek into the mountains and test the border granary before civilians were evacuated. A vigorous debate arose over whether the corrected map should be released immediately. A waterproof cover protected the pages, and the calculations were still legible. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 聽證後，員工終於能不受記者盤問地社交。 門禁紀錄涵蓋七小時，其中午夜附近有一段無法解釋的空白。 作者奉命徒步進入山區，在平民撤離前測試邊境穀倉。 對於是否立刻公開更正地圖，現場展開激烈辯論。 防水封套保存了紙頁，計算仍清晰可讀。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Iris had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Orren wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Hero Severin. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。伊莉絲知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。奧倫把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起英雄賽維林注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. Iris tests a temporary seal with volunteers who can stop the work whenever the strain grows dangerous.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 伊莉絲與志工測試臨時封印，所有人都能在負荷過高時停手。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. The results prove that no single person must be forced to suffer indefinitely.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 結果證明，沒有任何一個人必須被迫無限期承受痛苦。


## CHAPTER 10｜The Rift Was Cut Open｜裂隙遭人鑿開

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：865**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. Ten days after the recall, Iris finds a deliberate cut in the old seal network beneath the hero's victory pavilion. Iris knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。罷免十天後，伊莉絲在英雄慶功亭下方的舊封印網發現人為切口。伊莉絲知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** Iris crawled beneath the victory pavilion with a lantern tied to her belt. The cut in the seal line was too straight to have come from an earthquake. Orren, watching from the safe side of the barrier, pointed out a groove made by a particular two-pronged instrument. Only the hero's armory kept such tools. Iris did not accuse Severin immediately; she called two engineers who had no reason to defend the former king. Both described the same mark independently. When Severin saw their drawings, he did not ask how to repair the seal. He asked who had given them access to his private equipment. Iris understood that the danger outside the wall had an accomplice inside the government, and she sealed the tunnel before he could order it cleaned.

**繁中** 伊莉絲把提燈綁在腰際，爬到慶功亭底下。封印線上的切口過於筆直，不可能是地震造成。站在安全側的奧倫指出，那是某種雙叉工具留下的凹槽，而同款工具只存放於英雄的軍械庫。伊莉絲沒有立刻指控賽維林，而是找來兩名沒有理由袒護前魔王的工程師。兩人獨立描述出相同痕跡。賽維林看見他們的圖紙時，沒有問怎麼修封印，反而問誰准他們接觸私人設備。伊莉絲明白城牆外的危險在政府內部有幫手，立刻封閉隧道，阻止他下令清理現場。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The supplier sought a concession that would grant it control over the canal for a decade. A cynical investor said patients were useful only when their stories moved the market. The old procurement rule treated silence as consent by default, which the new board rejected. The chancellor's lawyer made a sharp retort about national morale, claiming that questioning the Sun Engine would deplete public confidence. Soldiers handed out sweet buns, and the fragrance of crushed flowers covered the smell of oil along the river. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 供應商尋求特許權，想控制運河十年。 一位憤世嫉俗的投資人說，病人故事只有能推動市場時才有用。 舊採購規則預設沉默即同意，新董事會予以否決。 宰相律師尖銳反駁，談起國民士氣，聲稱質疑太陽引擎會耗盡公眾信心。 士兵發放甜麵包，壓碎花朵的香氣蓋住河邊油味。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. The tool mark matches an instrument in Severin’s private armory, turning a natural disaster into a sabotage inquiry. Iris felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。工具痕跡吻合賽維林私人軍械庫裡的器械，天然災難因此轉成蓄意破壞調查。伊莉絲一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: No official would be permitted to intrude on protected witness meetings without a court order. A young jockey delivered a sealed message after taking the fastest road beside the racetrack. The clerk recorded the literal wording of the ownership claim without adding her opinion. The chancellor's lawyer made a sharp retort about national morale, claiming that questioning the border granary would deplete public confidence. The court could prosecute fraud only after evidence met the required standard. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 沒有法院命令，任何官員都不得闖入受保護的證人會面。 一名年輕騎師沿賽馬場旁最快的路送來封口信件。 書記逐字記下所有權主張，沒有加入個人意見。 宰相律師尖銳反駁，談起國民士氣，聲稱質疑邊境穀倉會耗盡公眾信心。 證據達到法定標準後，法庭才能起訴詐欺。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Iris insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Orren drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。伊莉絲堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。奧倫畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The maintenance inspector checked for radioactive materials because an old sensor carried a misleading label. The chancellor's lawyer made a sharp retort about national morale, claiming that questioning the prophecy relay would deplete public confidence. A young courier came forward with a screwdriver and a box of broken clock parts. Outside, an elderly vendor offered each departing witness a serving of warm soup. The tower's splendor seemed irrelevant beside the dark apartments across the river. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 維修檢查員因舊感測器標示誤導而檢查有無放射性物質。 宰相律師尖銳反駁，談起國民士氣，聲稱質疑預言中繼站會耗盡公眾信心。 一名年輕信差帶著螺絲起子和一箱壞時鐘零件上前。 庭外，一名年長攤販給離開的每位證人一份熱湯。 相較河對岸漆黑的公寓，大樓的華麗顯得毫無意義。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A spontaneous murmur passed through the audience. The boy began to stutter when he saw his stepfather at the back of the hall. The nervous clerk began to stutter before the judge invited her to speak slowly. The city agreed to subsidize emergency batteries for low-income patients during the trial. The military had claimed its newest heat reservoirs contained harmless synthetic stone. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 旁聽席傳出自發的低語。 少年在庭後看見繼父，說話開始結巴。 緊張的書記開始口吃，法官請她慢慢說。 市府同意在試辦期間補助低收入病人的緊急電池。 軍方聲稱最新儲能庫採用無害的合成石材；學院在丟棄的隔熱材料中發現少量放射性礦物痕跡，但含量不足以確認疾病原因。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Iris passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Orren paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，伊莉絲經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。奧倫停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。
