### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. Ten days after the recall, Iris finds a deliberate cut in the old seal network beneath the hero's victory pavilion.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 罷免十天後，伊莉絲在英雄慶功亭下方的舊封印網發現人為切口。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The tool mark matches an instrument in Severin’s private armory, turning a natural disaster into a sabotage inquiry.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 工具痕跡吻合賽維林私人軍械庫裡的器械，天然災難因此轉成蓄意破壞調查。


## CHAPTER 11｜The Hero's Loyalists｜英雄的忠誠者

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：826**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. Severin blames Orren and orders all former palace workers detained without individual charges. Iris had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the city of Blackgate, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。賽維林指控奧倫，命令將所有舊宮員工不分罪責拘押。伊莉絲工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在黑門城，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Iris could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Orren suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，伊莉絲無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。奧倫提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Lira brought a thermometer calibrated in Celsius and a probe designed to measure changes in river heat. The machine's upper wheel turned slowly because friction had damaged its bearings. A stray dog began to growl when inspectors approached the abandoned loading dock. The lawyer said a forged instruction could induce workers to override a safe setting. The fishermen who inhabit these shores would need a permanent place on the safety board. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 伊莉絲帶來以攝氏度校準的溫度計，以及測量河水熱能變化的探針。 機器上方的輪子轉得很慢，因為摩擦已損傷軸承。 檢查員靠近廢置裝貨區時，一隻流浪狗開始低吼。 律師說偽造指令可能誘使工人覆寫安全設定。 居住在這片海岸的漁民需要在安全委員會中擁有永久席次。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. Iris refuses to enforce the order and calls an emergency assembly of district delegates. Orren asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Iris agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。伊莉絲拒絕執行，召集各區代表舉行緊急會議。奧倫詢問是否可能只是出錯，伊莉絲也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: It was ironic that the trust's security account had been active long after its owner died. A retired mechanic lived on a grassy mound near the station. A peacock from a nearby garden wandered close to the temporary generator fence. A pianist played for patients while the clinic's backup system was tested. Nessa used a narrow probe to inspect the edge of the plate without touching the engraved letters. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 諷刺的是，信託保全帳戶在原持有人死後很久仍活躍。 一名退休技師住在測站附近的草丘上。 附近花園的一隻孔雀走近臨時發電機圍欄。 診所測試備電系統時，一位鋼琴家為病人演奏。 妮莎用細探針檢查印版邊緣，沒有碰到刻字。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Hero Severin demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Iris replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當英雄賽維林要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。伊莉絲回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The respective duties of the city and company had been confused in the old emergency plan. Lira admitted that her previous shortsighted policy had made a second approval optional. Maelin tried to simplify the public explanation without removing the legal qualification from the original. The oldest Sun Engine stood under a narrow strait, its copper pipes concealed in the foundation of an abandoned customs house. The superficial inspection had missed heat damage deep inside the relay cabinet. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 舊緊急計畫混淆了市府與公司的各自責任。 莉拉承認過去短視的政策使第二道核准變成可選。 伊莉絲試圖簡化公開說明，但不刪去原文的法律限定語。 最古老的太陽引擎位於狹窄海峽下方，銅管藏在廢棄海關屋的地基裡。 表面的檢查漏掉了繼電器櫃內部深處的熱損傷。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: No officer was allowed to suppress a witness statement merely because it contradicted the palace account. The symmetry of the instruments mattered because each team had to measure the same change independently. An expired access token had been used to send the dormant account's last message. The public committee tried to unify the three separate emergency notification systems. The nurse emphasized the urgency of restoring the clinic's refrigerated medical supply. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 任何官員都不得只因證詞與王宮說法相反就壓下證言。 儀器的對稱配置很重要，因為每組人都必須獨立測量同一項變化。 最後一封休眠帳戶訊息使用了一枚過期存取憑證。 公共委員會試圖整合三套分離的緊急通知系統。 護士強調恢復診所冷藏醫療用品供應的急迫性。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Iris resisted the urge to call it proof. Orren opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。伊莉絲克制住把它當成證明的衝動。奧倫翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Severin blames Orren and orders all former palace workers detained without individual charges.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 賽維林指控奧倫，命令將所有舊宮員工不分罪責拘押。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. Iris refuses to enforce the order and calls an emergency assembly of district delegates.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 伊莉絲拒絕執行，召集各區代表舉行緊急會議。


## CHAPTER 12｜The School Beneath the Gate｜城門下的學校

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：858**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. A school built inside an old tunnel provides records of repairs conducted by children’s families for generations. Iris saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。城門下的學校保留了數代家庭共同維護裂隙的紀錄。伊莉絲明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Iris asked Orren not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。伊莉絲請奧倫不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A small brook flowed behind the pump house and concealed part of its old foundation. The canal rescue team removed fragile coral samples from a science display before flooding. Lira refused to deprive residents of access while the lease remained in dispute. Workers used a platform to elevate the backup batteries above the projected flood line. The lease had an expiration date, but no one could find its required public notice. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 抽水站後方的小溪遮住一部分舊地基。 洪水前，運河救援隊移走科學展示裡脆弱的珊瑚樣本。 租約仍有爭議時，莉拉拒絕剝奪居民的通行權。 工人用平台把備用電池抬高到預測淹水線以上。 租約有到期日，卻沒有人找得到法定公開通知。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. The records reveal that the first demon king was appointed as a custodian, not born as a ruler. Iris watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Orren recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。紀錄顯示第一位魔王原是受託守護者，並非天生命定的統治者。伊莉絲看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。奧倫公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The retired mechanic's failing eyesight made written testimony easier than reading small diagrams. A hearty meal was prepared for everyone stranded by the storm, including the visiting officials. A rescue bird seemed to hover above the water gate as the current rose. The inland authorities had demanded fees for boats using an island landing they did not own. The lawyer's insistence on public notice changed the entire lease negotiation. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 退休技師視力衰退，書面作證比閱讀小圖更容易。 為所有因暴風雨受困的人準備了豐盛餐點，包括外來官員。 水位上漲時，一隻救援鳥似乎在水閘上方盤旋。 內陸當局曾向使用其無所有權島岸碼頭的船隻收費。 律師堅持公開通知，改變了整場租約談判。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Iris had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Orren proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 伊莉絲原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。奧倫提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The vault displayed a coral ornament that had never been included in its lease. A visiting legislator asked whether the emergency access rule was enforceable after the trial. The majestic river painting concealed a modest note written by the original marsh owner. Anika used the storm's momentum to persuade rival officers to open a shared shelter. The monarch's seal appeared on the contract, but nobody could locate her signed approval. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 密庫展示一件從未列入租約的珊瑚飾物。 來訪議員詢問試辦結束後，緊急通行規則是否仍可執行。 雄偉河景畫下藏著原沼地所有者寫的簡短註記。 安妮卡利用風暴帶來的迫切感，說服對立官員開放共用避難所。 契約上有君主印章，卻無人找到她親筆核准。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: An animal shelter asked for reliable heating to protect newborn offspring during power cuts. A petrol generator was retained as a backup only after its emissions were tested. The operator had to rotate the valve slowly to prevent a sudden pressure change. Maelin warned that the false fee might strangle the island's small repair trade over time. The physician explained that exhausted muscles could fail during a critical spell, just as poor surgical instruments could fail in an emergency. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 動物收容所要求可靠暖氣，以保護圍城期間剛出生的幼獸。 汽油發電機通過排放測試後才被保留作備用。 操作員必須緩慢旋轉閥門，以免壓力突然變化。 伊莉絲警告，不實收費長期可能扼殺島上小型修船業。 醫師解釋，疲憊肌肉可能在關鍵施法時失效，就像不良外科器械在急救時會出問題。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Iris returned to a broken black seal. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Orren remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the city of Blackgate waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，伊莉絲再次看向一枚裂開的黑色封印。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。奧倫守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的黑門城等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

   - B. A school built inside an old tunnel provides records of repairs conducted by children’s families for generations.

## CHAPTER 13｜A Burden with Twelve Handles｜有十二個握把的負荷

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：854**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Iris refused to begin with the official conclusion. Engineers design a new seal that allows twelve communities to share the strain and inspect one another. In the city of Blackgate, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。伊莉絲拒絕從官方結論開始。工程師設計新的封印，讓十二個社區分擔負荷並彼此檢查。在黑門城，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Iris listened longer than the schedule allowed. Orren reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。伊莉絲聽得比議程允許的更久。奧倫提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An accountant used basic algebra to explain how the reserve funds had been divided. The technician said the antonym of 'authorized' was not automatically 'criminal'. Engineers had been told to conserve fuel in the capital by drawing warmth from border rivers instead. The librarian was an outgoing woman who kept a discreet inventory of each volume sent for repair. A servant brought dressing for a simple supper salad, then apologized for interrupting the investigation. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 會計師用基礎代數說明準備金如何被拆分。 技師說「獲授權」的反義詞不會自動等於「犯罪」。 工程師奉命節省京城燃料，改從邊境河流抽取熱量。 圖書管理員性格外向，卻謹慎記錄每本送修書籍的清單。 一名侍者送來簡單晚餐沙拉的醬汁，為打斷調查道歉。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. One community declines, forcing Iris to redesign rather than compel participation. Iris read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Orren did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。其中一區拒絕加入，伊莉絲只得重新設計，不能強迫參與。伊莉絲把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。奧倫沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: At dusk, they examined a sequence of small drawings printed beside the equations. The palace encyclopedia repeated a claim that the children's recordings had already disproved. The memory of her own careless signature would haunt Maelin even if the tribunal cleared her of treason. The servant's voice carried no bitterness, only a fatigue born from official indifference. The clerk entered inventory in the testimony without deciding what it proved. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 黃昏時，他們檢查方程式旁的一連串小圖。 王宮百科全書重複了兒童錄音早已反駁的說法。 即使法庭洗清叛國罪，自己草率簽名的記憶仍會縈繞伊莉絲。 侍者沒有怨恨，只有官方冷漠造成的疲憊。 書記將「inventory」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Hero Severin and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Iris rejected both. The issue was who carries the burden of keeping the world intact, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住英雄賽維林的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。伊莉絲兩者都拒絕。核心是誰有責任承擔維持世界完整的代價，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A mother sang a lullaby in the waiting room while investigators examined the scanner files. The witness used the term outgoing in the report, but the team checked the actual event instead of relying on labels. At the community meeting, former pupils outnumber the museum officials by three to one. The outward appearance of the two treaty books was similar, while their inner clauses differed. Security staff were told to restrain angry visitors only if someone faced immediate danger. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 調查員檢查掃描檔時，一名母親在候診室唱搖籃曲。 證人在報告中使用「outgoing」一詞，團隊仍然核查真實事件，而非只依賴標籤。 社群會議中，舊學生人數是館方官員的三倍。 兩份條約冊外表相似，內部條文卻不同。 保全被告知只有發生立即危險時才能約束憤怒訪客。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: One illustrated page honored a sportswoman whose sportsmanship had helped rival villages organize a shared winter race. The clerk entered sportsmanship in the testimony without deciding what it proved. The assistant left her suitcase with security before entering the protected interview room. The judge issued a tentative finding that the word meant custody, subject to expert review. The review was underway before the palace press office could issue another false summary. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 其中一頁插圖表彰一位女運動員，她展現的運動家精神曾協助敵對村莊合辦冬季賽事。 書記將「sportsmanship」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 助理進入受保護訪談室前，把行李箱交給保全。 法官提出暫時認定：該詞意為保管，但仍須專家複查。 王宮新聞室再次發布錯誤摘要前，審查已經展開。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Iris had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Orren wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Hero Severin. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。伊莉絲知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。奧倫把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起英雄賽維林注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. Engineers design a new seal that allows twelve communities to share the strain and inspect one another.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 工程師設計新的封印，讓十二個社區分擔負荷並彼此檢查。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. One community declines, forcing Iris to redesign rather than compel participation.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 其中一區拒絕加入，伊莉絲只得重新設計，不能強迫參與。


## CHAPTER 14｜The King Who Hid the Truth｜隱瞞真相的魔王

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：853**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Orren confesses that he concealed the maintenance cost to keep hostile factions from weaponizing the rift. Iris asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。奧倫承認隱瞞維護成本，是為了不讓敵對勢力把裂隙當武器。伊莉絲請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** Orren arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Iris could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 奧倫帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。伊莉絲感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The admiral had inquired about the change and received an instruction bearing the chancellor's seal. A traveler led investigators astray by repeating a rumor, then corrected himself when shown the actual path. The workers' brotherhood was built through shared safety practices rather than blind loyalty. A clinic asked the power team to protect refrigeration for patients with diabetes. The court needed to differentiate a signature proving receipt from one authorizing a change of wording. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 上將曾詢問為何改道，得到一份蓋有宰相印的指令。 一名旅人重述流言而使調查員走錯路，看到真實路線後便更正。 工人間的兄弟情誼建立在共同安全實踐，而非盲目忠誠。 診所要求供電團隊保護糖尿病患者相關冷藏設備。 法庭必須區分證明收件的簽名與授權改字的簽名。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. Iris tells him that good intentions never gave him the right to deny citizens informed consent. For a long moment no one spoke. Iris understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Orren broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。伊莉絲告訴他，善意也不能剝奪人民知情同意的權利。一時之間無人開口。伊莉絲明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。奧倫打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A later message told him that questioning it would bring doom upon his command. Excess water collected in the underpass, so the search was postponed until engineers approved entry. The judges considered it feasible to secure the documents without exposing workers to the flooded passage. A feminine style of handwriting was noted in one margin, but Maelin warned against guessing the writer's identity. It was imperative to preserve the original file before examining any copies. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 後來另有消息威脅，若再質疑，整支艦隊便會遭厄運。 通道積水過多，搜索因此延後，直到工程師准許進入。 法官認為可以在不讓工人冒險進入淹水通道的情況下保全文件。 一處頁邊字跡看似女性筆法，伊莉絲卻警告不可據此猜作者身分。 檢查任何副本前，保存原始檔至關重要。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Iris insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Orren drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。伊莉絲堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。奧倫畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The panel warned that a hasty verdict could inflict harm on innocent employees. Halda asked the contractor to inquire whether his company had kept duplicate keys. An archival photograph showed a clinic that had once offered psychotherapy to displaced families. Keir measured the radius of the old harbor turning circle to check whether the delivery wagon could pass. The admiral said no reign should be remembered only through the medals displayed in its halls. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 小組警告倉促判決可能傷害無辜員工。 哈爾妲請承包商查詢公司是否保留備鑰。 檔案照片顯示一間曾替流離家庭提供心理治療的診所。 凱爾測量舊港迴轉圈半徑，確認運貨車能否通過。 海軍上將說，任何一朝統治都不應只由大廳陳列的勳章來被記住。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The audit identified a serious shortcoming in the emergency bypass approval process. Lira told the workers to tuck loose cables safely away from the moving gate. The underpass near the canal would close whenever the flood warning reached the red level. A dim underpass connected the market warehouse to the old wharf beneath the city wall. Iva described the woe caused by a border rule that treated her neighbors' homes as cargo. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 稽核指出緊急繞過授權流程有一項嚴重缺陷。 莉拉叫工人把鬆散電線收妥，遠離活動閘門。 運河旁地下通道在洪水警報達紅色時必須封閉。 昏暗地下通道把市場倉庫連到城牆下的舊碼頭。 伊娃描述邊界規定把鄰居家園當貨物所造成的悲苦。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Iris passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Orren paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，伊莉絲經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。奧倫停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Orren confesses that he concealed the maintenance cost to keep hostile factions from weaponizing the rift.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 奧倫承認隱瞞維護成本，是為了不讓敵對勢力把裂隙當武器。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. Iris tells him that good intentions never gave him the right to deny citizens informed consent.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 伊莉絲告訴他，善意也不能剝奪人民知情同意的權利。


## CHAPTER 15｜A Night of Open Doors｜敞開大門的一夜

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：830**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. District residents shelter former enemies as cracks spread through the market and the southern bridge. Iris checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。裂縫蔓延市場與南橋，各區居民開始收容昔日敵人。伊莉絲核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Iris asked Orren to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。伊莉絲請奧倫說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Merchants and village councils had formed an affiliate network to support witnesses, while young apprentices aspired to train as independent safety examiners. Lira said she would always aspire to build a company that could survive criticism. The canal district stood on the brink of another dangerous flood when the warning arrived. In the courtyard, a sparrow hopped beside a ceramic bowl of water. Crowds began to chant for an immediate verdict, but the council chair insisted on completing review. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 商人與村議會結成支援證人的附屬網絡，年輕學徒則希望受訓成為獨立安全檢驗員。 莉拉說她會一直渴望建立一家能承受批評的公司。 警報送達時，運河區正處於另一場危險洪水的邊緣。 」伊莉絲擔心群眾的興奮會分散對證據的注意。 群眾開始高喊要求立即判決，議長卻堅持完成審查。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. A family once punished by Orren’s taxes volunteers to operate the first shared seal. Iris felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。曾因奧倫稅制受苦的家庭，自願操作第一枚共享封印。伊莉絲一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A science museum's chimpanzee exhibit reminded visiting students that cleverness is not accountability. The staff received a meal coupon instead of the full wages they were owed. An elaborate publicity event would be a distraction from the incomplete engineering test. The independent examiner asked for two separate witnesses to verify the transfer signature. Lira had an instinctive urge to blame her uncle, but she waited for evidence. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 科學博物館的黑猩猩展示提醒學生，聰明不等於負責。 員工拿到餐券，卻沒有領到應得的全部薪水。 華麗的公關活動只會分散對未完成工程測試的注意。 獨立審查員要求兩名分開的證人驗證轉帳簽名。 莉拉本能地想責怪叔父，卻選擇等待證據。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Hero Severin demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Iris replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當英雄賽維林要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。伊莉絲回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Rare wooden frames would perish if the heating system failed during a damp winter. The committee would ratify the new timetable only after the engineers approved its safety threshold. An investor's reckless demand for immediate reopening was rejected by the worker committee. A technician had tried to reproduce the freezing pattern in a controlled chamber, using the same equipment but a much smaller reservoir. A palace sergeant testified that no official had ordered the workers' peaceful meeting dispersed. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 潮濕冬天若供暖系統故障，珍貴木框可能毀壞。 工程師確認安全門檻後，委員會才會正式批准新時程。 一名投資人魯莽要求立即重啟，遭工人委員會拒絕。 一名技師用相同設備與較小的儲能庫，在受控艙室嘗試重現凍結模式。 王宮中士作證，沒官員下令驅散工人的和平集會。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A sparrow settled on the office railing while Lira reviewed the storm map. A newspaper subscription paid for by the trust had been used to circulate misleading reports. The council postponed ratification, and thereafter every delegate could propose a correction in writing. The company funded vocational training for workers who needed new equipment qualifications. A community yoga class moved indoors when the wind warning was raised. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 莉拉檢視風暴地圖時，一隻麻雀停在辦公室欄杆上。 信託支付的一份報紙訂閱被用來傳播誤導報導。 議會延後追認，此後每位代表都可書面提出更正。 公司資助需要新設備資格的工人接受職業訓練。 風警升級時，一堂社區瑜伽課改到室內。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Iris resisted the urge to call it proof. Orren opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。伊莉絲克制住把它當成證明的衝動。奧倫翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

## CHAPTER 16｜The Recall of the Hero｜英雄的罷免案

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：837**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. The council debates removing Severin for his illegal arrests, but the city refuses to abandon due process. Iris knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。議會討論因非法拘捕而罷免賽維林，城市拒絕放棄正當程序。伊莉絲知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Iris could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Orren suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，伊莉絲無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。奧倫提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A volunteer with elegant calligraphy labeled boxes holding the workers' emergency records. A porter helped move heavy medical boxes to the shelter, their cartons packed beneath cardboard covers. The conservators placed the old wrapping in a labeled carton for later analysis. The city's infirmaries had prepared to cater to travelers during a festival, not a sudden loss of heat. Doctors monitored vulnerable patients, including those who needed specialized diets to control cholesterol. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一名書法漂亮的志工為裝有工人緊急紀錄的箱子寫標籤。 一名搬運工協助把沉重醫療箱送到避難所，紙箱外包著硬紙板。 修復員把舊包裝放進有標籤的紙箱，留待分析。 城裡診所原本準備接待節慶旅人，沒想到會突然失去供暖。 醫師監測脆弱病患，包括需要特殊飲食控制膽固醇的人。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. An independent engineer confirms the sabotage mark without yet identifying the person who wielded the tool. Orren asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Iris agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。獨立工程師確認人為破壞痕跡，但尚未查出實際動手者。奧倫詢問是否可能只是出錯，伊莉絲也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Two nights after the hearing, a disturbance swept through the central heat network. Warning lamps flickered and several reservoirs began to cool downward toward dangerous levels. An indignant merchant complained that his private banquet had been postponed. The apprentice called the plate-making technique a marvel, while Nessa reminded him that skill could be misused. Their first task was to mediate a dispute between military guards and civilian operators about who could enter the control room. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 聽證兩夜後，中央熱能網發生異常。 警示燈忽明忽暗，數座儲能庫的溫度一路下降到危險範圍。 一名憤憤不平的商人抱怨私人宴會被延期。 學徒讚嘆製版技術是奇蹟，妮莎提醒他技術也可能被濫用。 第一項任務，是調解軍方守衛與民間操作員對控制室進入權的爭執。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Iris had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Orren proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 伊莉絲原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。奧倫提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: There was nothing miraculous about the effort, only preparation and persistent work. A village apprentice carved a paddle showing the same crescent mark as the old seal. Iris's persistent questions exposed missing signatures in the fast-track register. The station porter carried the sealed boxes only after signing the custody log. A volunteer used clean water filters to purify supplies and checked that the kitchen could serve people with allergies. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 這番努力毫無神蹟可言，只有事前準備和持續工作。 村中學徒雕了一支船槳，上面有與舊印章相同的新月記號。 瑪拉持續追問，揭露快速登記冊缺少簽名。 車站搬運工簽署保管紀錄後才搬動封箱。 志工以乾淨的濾水器淨化物資，確認廚房能提供過敏者適合的餐點。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Lira arrived with a training team that had rehearsed a safe shutdown at the academy. Halda used a messenger to relay the council's decision without surrendering the original document. An orderly supply of stationery helped volunteers label and track every sealed package. A superstitious driver hesitated at the old tunnel, but followed the approved route. Inside, a broken walnut-colored handle had jammed the manual switch. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 伊莉絲帶著曾在學院排練安全停機的訓練隊抵達。 哈爾妲透過信使轉達議會決定，沒有交出原件。 文具供應有序，協助志工標記追蹤每份封存物品。 一位迷信的司機在舊隧道前猶豫，仍依核准路線行駛。 裡面一個胡桃木色的手柄卡住手動開關。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Iris returned to a broken black seal. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Orren remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the city of Blackgate waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，伊莉絲再次看向一枚裂開的黑色封印。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。奧倫守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的黑門城等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. The council debates removing Severin for his illegal arrests, but the city refuses to abandon due process.

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. An independent engineer confirms the sabotage mark without yet identifying the person who wielded the tool.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 獨立工程師確認人為破壞痕跡，但尚未查出實際動手者。


## CHAPTER 17｜The Night Market Treaty｜夜市條約

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：848**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. Humans, demons, and displaced merchants negotiate access to tunnels needed for the new network. Iris had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the city of Blackgate, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。人類、魔族與流離商人談判取得新網路需要的隧道通行權。伊莉絲工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在黑門城，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Iris asked Orren not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。伊莉絲請奧倫不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An analytical report separated the storm's direct damage from earlier maintenance failures. Chancellor Varr answered the sabotage report with a campaign to boycott the academy and its suppliers. The staff council refused to treat a wounded worker as an acceptable casualty of the inquiry. A nurse offered consolation to a family whose refrigerator had failed again. The missing correspondence was recovered from the carton with the false bottom. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一份分析報告將風暴直接損失與先前維修失誤分開。 瓦爾宰相用抵制學院及其供應商的運動回應破壞報告。 員工委員會拒絕把受傷工人當成調查可接受的犧牲。 一名護士安慰冰箱再次故障的家庭。 失蹤往來書信從有假底的紙箱中找回。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. The agreement recognizes that physical safety and political rights cannot be traded against one another. Iris watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Orren recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。協議承認人身安全與政治權利不能互相交換。伊莉絲看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。奧倫公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: At a refugee center, volunteers were cultivating vegetables in a courtyard once used for weapons storage. The company agreed to disclosure of past maintenance failures under an independent protocol. Maelin feared that a single hidden error might survive for eternity if later generations copied it blindly. An operator feared he would flunk the new training test, so the team scheduled practice. A nurse explained new hygiene rules after several children caught a seasonal illness. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 一處難民中心的志工正在曾存放武器的院子裡種菜。 公司同意依獨立程序揭露過去維修失誤。 伊莉絲擔心若後代盲目照抄，一項隱藏錯誤可能永遠存在。 一名操作員擔心新訓練測試不及格，團隊安排練習。 護理師解釋新的衛生規定，因為幾名孩子感染季節性疾病。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Hero Severin and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Iris rejected both. The issue was who carries the burden of keeping the world intact, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住英雄賽維林的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。伊莉絲兩者都拒絕。核心是誰有責任承擔維持世界完整的代價，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The nurse hoped that patient explanations would kindle trust among families who had long feared officials. The printer's workshop layout showed a back door near the route recorded in the carrier's ledger. The recovered reserve might be lucrative for investors, but its first duty was public repair. The outward sign of the guild looked respectable, although its purchasing records required close examination. A preventive order protected the archive doors against removal of materials without a signed inventory. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 護理師希望耐心的解說能在長年害怕官員的家庭之間重新點燃信任。 印刷坊平面配置圖顯示後門靠近信差帳冊所載路線。 追回準備金可能讓投資人獲利，但首要用途是公共修復。 公會外部招牌看來體面，但採購紀錄仍需嚴格審查。 預防性命令禁止任何人未附簽署清單就移出檔案室物品。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A child gave a shriek when the temporary siren sounded during a supervised test. The old courier began to sneeze from dust, and the court permitted a short break. The team measured electrical stimulation only on equipment designed to receive it. The repeated verification was tiresome, but the witnesses preferred it to another silent mistake. The technician had to wrestle with a rusted cabinet door before opening it safely. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 受監督的測試中，暫時警報器響起時，一名孩子尖叫。 老信差因灰塵開始打噴嚏，法庭准許短暫休息。 團隊只對設計可接受電刺激的設備測量該項數值。 反覆核驗令人疲倦，證人卻寧願如此，也不願再有默默發生的錯誤。 技師必須費力對付生鏽櫃門，才得以安全打開。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Iris had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Orren wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Hero Severin. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。伊莉絲知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。奧倫把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起英雄賽維林注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

## CHAPTER 18｜The Hero's Missing Hour｜英雄失蹤的一小時

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：857**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Iris finds a gap in Severin’s public schedule and three different witnesses who saw his guards under the pavilion. Iris saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。伊莉絲找到賽維林公開行程的一小時空白，三名證人都曾看見他的守衛在慶功亭下。伊莉絲明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Iris listened longer than the schedule allowed. Orren reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。伊莉絲聽得比議程允許的更久。奧倫提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The guard waited awhile before reporting the broken lock because he feared punishment. The day's evidence would broaden the inquiry beyond the warehouse to the people financing the operation. A volunteer from the western cape had traveled overnight to support flood relief. A captive audience at the shareholder meeting deserved more than a polished speech. The clerk mistook a consonant in the old access code and had to check the original. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 警衛因害怕處罰，等了一段時間才通報壞鎖。 當天的證據將把調查從倉庫擴大到出資安排整項行動的人。 一名西岬志工連夜趕來支援救災。 股東會上無法離席的聽眾值得聽到的不只是漂亮演說。 書記看錯舊門禁代碼的一個子音字母，只好查核原件。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. Severin denies ordering sabotage, but admits he paid for a demonstration meant to frighten voters. Iris read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Orren did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。賽維林否認下令破壞，卻承認曾出錢安排恐嚇選民的演示。伊莉絲把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。奧倫沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The palace could not dictate an unlimited closure when the signed originals required review dates. The tiny repair mark seemed to dwarf the importance of the grand royal seal. The museum released an excerpt of the court order while protecting private addresses. An intruder had recently tried to destroy the files, leaving a scorched corner on one shelf. A mischievous intern joked about ghosts in the server room before seeing the serious evidence. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 簽署原件要求複查日期，王宮便不能命令無期限封閉。 小小修復記號竟讓宏大的王室印章顯得不再重要。 博物館公開法庭命令節錄，同時保護私人地址。 最近有人闖入企圖銷毀文件，一個架子角落被燒焦。 一名調皮實習生看到嚴肅證據前，還開玩笑說伺服器室有鬼。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Iris insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Orren drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。伊莉絲堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。奧倫畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: After the storm weakened, an operative from the chancellor's office arrived claiming authority over the warehouse. The witness said the interrogation had been an ordeal but chose to finish her testimony. A dwarf engineer named Bran managed the pumps, assisted by several local workers who had once been classified as outlaws for refusing unpaid labor. A falling splinter could pinch a worker against the doorframe, so carpenters inspected the braces before anyone returned. The commander replied that wartime orders must precede civilian needs. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 風暴稍緩後，宰相辦公室的一名特派員到場，自稱有權接管倉庫。 證人說詢問過程是一場煎熬，仍選擇完成證詞。 一名矮人工程師布蘭管理抽水機，助手是幾位當年因拒絕無薪勞動而被列為不法之徒的居民。 掉落的木片可能把工人夾在門框旁，因此木匠先檢查支架，才讓人返回。 指揮官聲稱戰時命令必須優先於民間需求。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Restoration of the locked server room required both a physical repair and new access rules. A museum statute prevented the auction of objects with unresolved title claims. The weather office warned that a tornado was possible far outside the city's service region. The panel rejected wholesale seizure of every object handled by the same contractor. Keir used a magnifying lens to zoom in on the faded serial number of a harbor gate. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 修復上鎖的伺服器室需要實體維修與新門禁規則。 博物館法規禁止拍賣所有權尚未釐清的物件。 氣象單位警告城市服務範圍之外可能出現龍捲風。 小組拒絕一口氣全面扣押同一承包商碰過的所有物件。 凱爾用放大鏡放大查看港門褪色的序號。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Iris passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Orren paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，伊莉絲經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。奧倫停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. Iris finds a gap in Severin’s public schedule and three different witnesses who saw his guards under the pavilion.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 伊莉絲找到賽維林公開行程的一小時空白，三名證人都曾看見他的守衛在慶功亭下。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. Severin denies ordering sabotage, but admits he paid for a demonstration meant to frighten voters.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 賽維林否認下令破壞，卻承認曾出錢安排恐嚇選民的演示。


## CHAPTER 19｜The Final Anchor｜最後的錨點

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：848**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Iris refused to begin with the official conclusion. A storm opens the largest rift above the hospital, requiring every district to coordinate its seal at once. In the city of Blackgate, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。伊莉絲拒絕從官方結論開始。暴風雨使醫院上空裂開最大裂隙，所有區域必須同時協調封印。在黑門城，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** Orren arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Iris could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 奧倫帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。伊莉絲感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Local workers tried to fortify the temporary shelter before the next wave of rain. A former clerk met the investigators at the pier, wearing a glamorous coat that looked out of place beside the weathered docks. Investigators could infer a possible route from the door logs but not the intruder's identity. The witness's intonation changed when the lawyer mentioned the dormant account. A hurried announcement would invariably miss some facts still being verified. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 地方工人試圖在下一波雨前加固臨時避難所。 一名退休書記在碼頭迎接調查員，穿著與風化碼頭格格不入的華麗外套。 調查員可從門禁紀錄推論可能路線，卻不能推斷闖入者身分。 律師提到休眠帳戶時，證人的語調改變了。 倉促公告總會漏掉一些仍在查證的事實。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. Orren offers to carry the entire load again; Iris refuses to repeat the system that created the crisis. For a long moment no one spoke. Iris understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Orren broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。奧倫願獨自承擔所有負荷，伊莉絲卻拒絕重複製造危機的制度。一時之間無人開口。伊莉絲明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。奧倫打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The trail from Black Cape led to a small isle once used to store imperial correspondence. Lira found it difficult to meditate while waiting for the independent report. The island's tiny clinic served migrant fishermen as well as military families. The court reminded the crowd that every witness was mortal and could make mistakes. A nude figure in the old office mural had no connection with the damaged server. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 黑岬留下的線索通往一座小島，從前用來存放帝國往來公文。 一名移工說，欠薪使自己難以反對主管。 島上的小診所同時服務移居漁民與軍眷。 法庭提醒群眾，每名證人都是凡人，都可能犯錯。 舊辦公室壁畫中的裸體人像與損壞伺服器毫無關係。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Hero Severin demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Iris replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當英雄賽維林要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。伊莉絲回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The witness took an oath to tell the truth before answering questions about the purchase orders. A strange odor near the cabinet led engineers to test for overheated insulation. Halda called the suggestion that her village had invented its own history outrageous. A technician explained that a recent engine discharge had left a sharp smell like ozone near the water. Iris sealed the anonymous packet before asking an independent examiner to check its origin. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 證人在回答採購單問題前宣誓據實陳述。 櫃體旁異味促使工程師檢測絕緣材料是否過熱。 哈爾妲稱指控村落捏造自身歷史的說法荒謬至極。 技師解釋，最近一次引擎放能讓水邊留下近似臭氧的刺鼻氣味。 瑪拉封存匿名文件包，再請獨立鑑定人查來源。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The local navigators demonstrated proficiency in the dangerous channel that royal captains lacked. Lira refused to answer the whistleblower with scorn merely because his story changed. Investigators found no evidence that the courier had tried to smuggle batteries across the river. Halda crossed the hearing hall with a confident stride and placed the drawings before the judges. The corridor carried an odor of disinfectant and vanilla soap. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 在地引航者展現王室船長缺乏的危險水道熟練技術。 莉拉拒絕因吹哨者說法改變就輕蔑回應。 調查員沒發現快遞員試圖走私電池渡河的證據。 哈爾妲大步走過會堂，將描圖放到法官面前。 空氣裡有消毒劑與香草肥皂味。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Iris resisted the urge to call it proof. Orren opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。伊莉絲克制住把它當成證明的衝動。奧倫翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. A storm opens the largest rift above the hospital, requiring every district to coordinate its seal at once.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 暴風雨使醫院上空裂開最大裂隙，所有區域必須同時協調封印。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Orren offers to carry the entire load again; Iris refuses to repeat the system that created the crisis.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 奧倫願獨自承擔所有負荷，伊莉絲卻拒絕重複製造危機的制度。


## CHAPTER 20｜The Twelve Hands｜十二隻手

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：845**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Residents activate the shared seals while the council publicly tracks each participant’s safe limits. Iris asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。居民啟動共享封印，議會公開追蹤每位參與者的安全極限。伊莉絲請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Iris asked Orren to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。伊莉絲請奧倫說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The evacuation proceeded in accordance with the safety plan approved by the staff council. The clinic kept the lights on for a counseling session about adolescence during the reduction. A caterpillar crawled across the emergency map while volunteers worked outdoors. The safety officer told visitors to move clockwise around the inner courtyard. The rescue team used a compass when the floodwater covered familiar street markers. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 疏散依照員工委員會批准的安全計畫進行。 降載期間，診所仍維持一場青春期諮詢課的照明。 志工在室外工作時，一隻毛毛蟲爬過緊急地圖。 安全員請訪客順時針繞內庭移動。 洪水淹沒熟悉的路標時，救援隊使用指南針。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. The rift closes only after the final district chooses to join of its own free will. Iris felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。最後一區自願加入時，裂隙才終於閉合。伊莉絲一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The second relay reading did not contradict the first, but showed a dangerous delay. Chancellor Varr's lawyer raised a dubious objection, arguing that a monarch could exempt his own office from scrutiny. The ministry had used false figures to enhance its political standing as well as its budget. The witness used the term exempt in the report, but the team checked the actual event instead of relying on labels. Outside the hall, soldiers stood beside a field where horses grazed. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 第二次繼電器讀數並未與第一次矛盾，而是顯示危險延遲。 瓦爾律師提出可疑的異議，主張君主可以豁免自己的辦公室接受審查。 部門曾利用錯誤數字提高政治地位，同時增加預算。 證人在報告中使用「exempt」一詞，團隊仍然核查真實事件，而非只依賴標籤。 大廳外，士兵站在馬匹吃草的草地旁。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Iris had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Orren proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 伊莉絲原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。奧倫提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Lira had a hunch that the delay originated in a damaged connector, but ordered measurements. An engineer's intellect was useful only when paired with the discipline to stop unsafe work. A patriotic display once used nationalism to justify claiming every coastal object for the crown. A loose ceiling ornament fell after the western roof tiles were torn away. The river guard offered a salute to the visiting Hill Council delegates before the joint inspection. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 莉拉直覺延遲源於損壞接頭，仍下令實測。 工程師的才智只有配合停止危險作業的紀律才有用。 一個愛國展曾用民族主義合理化王室占有所有沿海物件。 西側屋瓦被掀開後，一件鬆脫天花裝飾掉落。 共同檢查前，河岸衛兵向來訪山地議會代表敬禮。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The planner admitted he had signed sloppy estimates without reading the supporting evidence. One solitary guard watched an elderly farmer tiptoe around a broken stair, then offered help without waiting for an order. Emergency supplies included staple foods for residents who could not leave their homes. The staff council could summon the contractor to explain the roof sensor failure. A child tried to tiptoe past the assembly count, but a volunteer gently called her back. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 他承認自己未讀佐證便簽署草率估算。 一名獨自值守的衛兵看見老農夫踮著腳避開破階梯，沒有等命令便主動幫忙。 緊急物資包含給無法離家的居民的主食。 員工委員會能傳喚承包商解釋屋頂感測器故障。 一個孩子想踮腳溜過集合點名，志工溫柔叫她回來。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Iris returned to a broken black seal. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Orren remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the city of Blackgate waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，伊莉絲再次看向一枚裂開的黑色封印。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。奧倫守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的黑門城等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。
