### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Residents activate the shared seals while the council publicly tracks each participant’s safe limits.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 居民啟動共享封印，議會公開追蹤每位參與者的安全極限。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The rift closes only after the final district chooses to join of its own free will.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 最後一區自願加入時，裂隙才終於閉合。


## CHAPTER 21｜A Trial Without a Legend｜不靠傳說的審判

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：858**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. Severin faces an independent hearing over the threats, unlawful detentions, and financing of the staged incident. Iris checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。賽維林因恐嚇、非法拘押和資助演示接受獨立審理。伊莉絲核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Iris could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Orren suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，伊莉絲無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。奧倫提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An aviation expert confirmed that the alleged emergency flight had never occurred. Beforehand, investigators had tested every copy of the island packet against original seals and accounting registers. Nessa prepared a booklet displaying the full original and the altered public summary side by side. The commission next heard from technicians who explained the cellular structure of the storage crystals. The duration of the deliberate outage had been calculated to trigger the trust clause. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 航空專家證實所謂的緊急航班根本沒有發生。 調查員事前已將島上文件包的每份副本與原始印璽、帳簿逐一核對。 妮莎準備一本小冊子，把完整原件與遭改公開摘要並排呈現。 委員會接著聽技師解釋儲能晶體的細胞狀結構。 蓄意圍城的持續時間經過計算，目的是觸發信託條款。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. The court separates proven misconduct from unproven accusations of causing the entire rift. Orren asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Iris agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。法院把已證實的違法行為與尚未證實的裂隙總責任分開。奧倫詢問是否可能只是出錯，伊莉絲也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A family folklore story about the founder could not replace the company's accounting records. No witness should have to endure harassment merely for appearing before the commission. There were villages marked in pencil where the imperial survey showed only blank wilderness. The imposing marble hall seemed less important than the elders waiting for their names to be heard. The treasurer testified that he had never been obliged to examine the final destination of the money. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 家族關於創辦人的民間故事不能取代公司帳目。 任何證人都不應只因出席委員會，就必須承受騷擾。 帝國測量圖上空白的荒野，在這張圖上卻用鉛筆標著村莊。 高大的大理石會堂，看起來不如等著姓名獲聽見的長者重要。 司庫作證說，過去沒有人要求他審查款項最終用途。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Hero Severin and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Iris rejected both. The issue was who carries the burden of keeping the world intact, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住英雄賽維林的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。伊莉絲兩者都拒絕。核心是誰有責任承擔維持世界完整的代價，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Vask seemed to obsess over the appearance of the seals rather than the actual changes in the words. An octopus painted on an old trading flag marked the shipping firm that carried the sealed plate case. Workers described how the ministry had used threats to oppress those who reported failures. The union organizer reminded witnesses that their private addresses would remain protected. Their report also identified a peril in continuing to operate the network without new safety rules. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 瓦斯克似乎過度執著於印章外觀，而非文字真正改動。 舊貿易旗上的章魚圖案標示了運送封存印版箱的船公司。 工人描述部門如何威脅回報故障的人，壓迫他們保持沉默。 工會組織者提醒證人，私人地址會受到保護。 報告也指出，若不訂新安全規則就繼續使用網路，將有重大危險。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A poultry farmer told the court that his family had lost livestock when their barn froze overnight. A prehistoric fossil in the courthouse display distracted a nervous child from the proceedings. Folklore could teach courage, but a proverb or story should never replace the documents needed to judge a living person's guilt. A model spacecraft stood in the lobby beside the city's new energy-efficiency exhibit. A wrinkle in the treasurer’s explanation emerged when the dates of two payment orders were compared. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 一名養禽農民告訴法庭，家裡的牲畜因穀倉整夜結凍而死。 法院展櫃裡的史前化石讓緊張孩子暫時轉移注意。 民間傳說能教人勇氣，但諺語和故事不能取代判斷活人罪責所需的文件。 大廳裡的太空船模型與城市新能源效率展示並列。 比較兩份付款命令日期時，司庫的說法出現一處破綻。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Iris had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Orren wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Hero Severin. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。伊莉絲知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。奧倫把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起英雄賽維林注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. Severin faces an independent hearing over the threats, unlawful detentions, and financing of the staged incident.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 賽維林因恐嚇、非法拘押和資助演示接受獨立審理。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The court separates proven misconduct from unproven accusations of causing the entire rift.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 法院把已證實的違法行為與尚未證實的裂隙總責任分開。


## CHAPTER 22｜The Seat That Stays Empty｜空著的座位

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：851**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. The assembly votes to keep the throne vacant and replace it with a rotating maintenance board. Iris knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。大會表決讓王座保持空缺，改設輪值的維護理事會。伊莉絲知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Iris asked Orren not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。伊莉絲請奧倫不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A choir rehearsed a new anthem about shared rivers rather than imperial conquest. Children would be able to ascend the hillside openly, while their parents continued to reside in their own villages. An independent financial audit revealed how wartime warehouses had received excessive funding for fuel and medicine. A retired captain who had once called eastern people barbarian offered an awkward apology. The manufacturer admitted a defect in one relay but denied causing the earlier sabotage. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 合唱團排練新的國歌，歌詞讚頌共同河流而非帝國征服。 孩子們將能公開登上山坡，父母也得以繼續住在自己的村莊。 獨立財務稽核揭露戰時倉庫如何為燃料與藥品取得過多經費。 一名退休上尉過去稱東方人為野蠻人，如今笨拙道歉。 製造商承認一個繼電器有缺陷，否認造成先前破壞。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Orren becomes an adviser who can be dismissed, not a king who cannot be questioned. Iris watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Orren recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。奧倫成為可被解聘的顧問，而非不可質疑的君王。伊莉絲看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。奧倫公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Some veterans grumbled that the change erased their sacrifices. Heavy hail damaged the archive roof, so certified copies were moved to a drier public building. The volunteer kitchen served healthful food without demanding that families support either side. A hedge of roses bordered the path where the two discussed their future work. The pharmacist confirmed that heroin was not part of the clinic's authorized medicine inventory. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 有老兵抱怨這抹去了他們的犧牲。 猛烈冰雹損壞檔案室屋頂，認證副本因此移往較乾燥的公共建物。 志工廚房提供健康餐點，不要求家庭支持任何一方。 兩人討論未來工作的小徑旁，有一道玫瑰樹籬。 藥師證實海洛因不在診所核准藥品庫存中。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Iris insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Orren drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。伊莉絲堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。奧倫畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Their intimacy did not erase their separate vocations. Delegates traveled from the mainland and the eastern federation to a hall outside North Gate, where they could mingle without crossing a military checkpoint. Workers could mingle with community delegates after the hearing without surveillance by their supervisors. The pharmacist testified that no illegal heroin shipment had passed the route, despite rumors used to discredit the witnesses. The negotiations began while a cold wind blew from the polar sea. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 他們的親密沒有抹去各自的志業，反而更容易說實話。 大陸與東方聯邦的代表來到北門外的會堂，在不經軍事檢查站的情況下交流。 聽證後工人能與社群代表交流，不受主管監視。 藥師作證說並無非法海洛因貨物經過該路線，反駁用來抹黑證人的流言。 停火談判開始時，寒風從極地海域吹來。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The old interpreter had chosen to reside near the ferry so he could hear everyday river speech. The agreement provided for regular reviews and a public complaint process resistant to political interference. A boy with a limp called to his stepmother to see the delegates arrive. The economist warned that a trillion imaginary crowns on a chart would not repair the real roof. A government did not become upright merely because it announced better intentions. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 老譯員選擇住在渡口附近，才能聽到日常河語。 協議訂有定期複查與不受政治干預的公開申訴程序。 一個跛腳男孩喊繼母來看代表抵達。 經濟學家警告，圖表上虛構的一兆枚王幣修不好真正的屋頂。 伊莉絲要求公開更正，並分別調查涉案者。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Iris passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Orren paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，伊莉絲經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。奧倫停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. The assembly votes to keep the throne vacant and replace it with a rotating maintenance board.

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Orren becomes an adviser who can be dismissed, not a king who cannot be questioned.

   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 奧倫成為可被解聘的顧問，而非不可質疑的君王。


## CHAPTER 23｜The Cost Made Public｜公開的代價

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：844**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. Iris releases every maintenance account, including the damage caused by Orren's past secrecy. Iris had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the city of Blackgate, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。伊莉絲公開全部維護帳目，包括奧倫過去隱瞞造成的傷害。伊莉絲工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在黑門城，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Iris listened longer than the schedule allowed. Orren reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。伊莉絲聽得比議程允許的更久。奧倫提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: To avert another wave of injustice, a commission of local representatives began reviewing claims in a public hall. The witness house caretaker provided an accurate list of the nights the courier had stayed there. Beneath a collapsed ceiling, inspectors found a cavity in the wall containing old ration tickets and labor lists. The legal clerk had to condense the technical report without removing disputed findings. The discovery caused dismay among officials who had insisted all records were lost. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 為避免另一波不義，地方代表組成委員會，在公共大廳審查申請。 證人屋管理人提供信差曾住宿各夜的準確名單。 倒塌屋頂下，檢查員發現牆裡有空腔，藏著舊配給券與工人名單。 法務書記必須濃縮技術報告，不能刪掉有爭議的發現。 這項發現使原先堅稱紀錄全失的官員驚慌。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. Families receive compensation and the right to challenge historical charges. Iris read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Orren did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。家庭獲得補償與對歷史徵收提出異議的權利。伊莉絲把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。奧倫沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: No one was forced into emigration to receive help, and no lottery determined who deserved a roof. One flawed military register had classified displaced residents as enemy casualties, exposing their families to intimidation when they requested compensation. Halda requested an inclusive meeting where young navigators and elders could both vote. Some former officers still tried to intimidate applicants, and the commission referred those threats for investigation. The officials would be liable for correcting records, not for protecting the prestige of her family. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 沒有人必須移民才能得到幫助，也沒有抽籤決定誰值得有屋頂。 一本有瑕疵的軍方登記冊曾把流離失所者列成敵方傷亡，使家屬申請補償時遭到威嚇。 哈爾妲要求包容性的會議，讓年輕引航者和長者都能投票。 部分前軍官仍企圖恐嚇申請者，委員會將這些威脅交付調查。 官員要負責更正紀錄，而不是保護王室聲望。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Hero Severin demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Iris replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當英雄賽維林要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。伊莉絲回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A charity lottery raised money for the bridge repairs without touching the disputed grain fund. A nearsighted clerk read each form carefully, checking for gaps rather than rushing. A child asked the clinic nurse about a pimple while her mother filed a power-loss claim. The revelation of the hidden rubbing connected the written order to the actual metal plate. An official had once ordered workers to shred duplicate records; several had refused and preserved them instead. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 慈善彩券為修橋募款，沒有動用有爭議的糧食基金。 一名近視書記仔細讀每份表格，找出缺口而不催促。 孩子問診所護士臉上的痘痘，母親則填寫圍城損失申請。 祕密拓印的揭露，把書面命令與實際金屬印版連接起來。 過去一名官員曾命工人碎毀副本紀錄；有幾人拒絕，反而把它們保存下來。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The solemn ruling gave his freedom a legal foundation that private promises had never offered. Blocking the road again would suffocate the town’s recovering trade, the local council warned. The repair fund faced a surge of applications after the compensation announcement. The engineers measured an upward movement in the gate during the final safety check. The rebuilding workforce included former soldiers and refugees, all paid under the same published rules. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 這項莊嚴判決為他的自由建立法律基礎，是私人承諾從未提供過的保障。 地方議會警告，再次封路會扼殺小鎮正在復甦的貿易。 賠償公告後，修復基金申請量激增。 工程師在最後安全檢查量到閘門向上移動。 重建勞動隊伍包括前士兵與難民，所有人都依同一套公開規則領薪。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Iris resisted the urge to call it proof. Orren opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。伊莉絲克制住把它當成證明的衝動。奧倫翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Iris releases every maintenance account, including the damage caused by Orren's past secrecy.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 伊莉絲公開全部維護帳目，包括奧倫過去隱瞞造成的傷害。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. Families receive compensation and the right to challenge historical charges.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 家庭獲得補償與對歷史徵收提出異議的權利。


## CHAPTER 24｜The Festival of Repairs｜修理的節日

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：889**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. The city celebrates its first repair festival rather than a military victory or a royal coronation. Iris saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。城市舉辦第一次修理節，而不是慶祝軍事勝利或君王加冕。伊莉絲明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** Orren arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Iris could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 奧倫帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。伊莉絲感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Accordingly, each region would receive a published timetable for its first safety audit. A warning device began to beep during the public demonstration, and the operator stopped the test. The chair reminded them that loyalty did not mean disbelief when evidence challenged official claims. Maelin rejected the idea that one kingdom had to civilize the others before their words deserved respect. A coastal cook served clam soup to delegates who had spent long hours arguing. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 因此，每個地區都將收到第一次安全稽核的公開時程。 公開示範時警示裝置開始發出嗶聲，操作員立刻停測。 有些代表愛國，為故鄉自豪；其他人則擔心愛國一詞再次被用來壓制批評。 伊莉絲反對某國必須先教化別國，其語言才值得尊重的想法。 一名海岸廚師端出蛤蜊湯，供應爭論多時的代表。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. A child asks why the festival honors people who once hated each other; Iris says they learned to share a burden. For a long moment no one spoke. Iris understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Orren broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。孩子問為何節日表揚過去互相憎恨的人，伊莉絲回答：因為他們學會共同承擔。一時之間無人開口。伊莉絲明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。奧倫打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Three months after the ceasefire, a coalition of eastern and western councils convened to formulate a permanent peace charter. A conscientious clerk recorded a complete transcript, and a simple bell gave one beep when each speaking period ended. The renovated reading room was cozy without hiding the building's history of neglect. Visitors used to devour romantic tales about royal collectors without questioning the labels. A disastrous design would never again be excused merely because its inventor held a royal title. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 停火三個月後，東西方議會組成聯盟，召開永久和平憲章會議。 她用簡短聲明開場：任何社區都不能被排除在自身河流的決策之外。 翻新的閱覽室溫馨舒適，也不隱藏建築過去遭忽視的歷史。 訪客過去常貪讀王室收藏家的浪漫故事，卻不質疑標籤。 災難性的設計不能再只因發明者擁有王室頭銜就獲得開脫。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Iris had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Orren proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 伊莉絲原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。奧倫提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The clerk entered disbelief in the testimony without deciding what it proved. The committee worked to formulate clear appeals rules before any contract was signed. Engineers from different latitudes explained why reservoirs near the northern mountains behaved differently from those in the warm tropic zones. Some delegates were patriotic and proud of their home territories; others feared that the word patriotism might again be used to silence criticism. The staff had to reckon with the cost of repairing the roof before reopening every gallery. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 書記將「disbelief」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 委員會在簽約前努力制定清楚的申訴規則。 不同緯度來的工程師解釋，北方山區的儲能庫與溫暖熱帶地區運作方式何以不同。 一名盡責書記記錄完整逐字稿，簡單的鈴聲在每段發言結束時發出一聲提示。 員工必須先計算屋頂修繕成本，才能開放所有展廳。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Black lines spread across a wall where a maintenance seal had cracked. Iris pressed her palm near the stone but did not touch it; the instrument showed that the surface was colder than the air. Repair was possible, yet each hour of neglect made the next repair more expensive.

**繁中** 維護封印裂開的牆上延伸出黑線。伊莉絲把手掌靠近石面卻沒有碰觸；儀器顯示石面比空氣更冷。修復並非不可能，但每疏忽一小時，下一次修繕就更加昂貴。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A river's ripple was visible through the windows, small and constant beneath the grand language of peace. The director's stature in the industry did not exempt him from independent inspection. The court released a transcript of the hearing after removing protected witness addresses. The clerk entered tropic in the testimony without deciding what it proved. Farmers described how a change in water temperature could affect crops, fish, and even the growth of a vine along river walls. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 窗外河面泛起細小而持續的漣漪，在宏大的和平語言之下安靜流動。 董事在業界的地位不能使他免於獨立檢查。 法院刪除受保護證人地址後，公布聽證逐字稿。 書記將「tropic」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 農民描述水溫改變如何影響作物、魚類，甚至河牆上藤蔓的生長。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Iris returned to a broken black seal. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Orren remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the city of Blackgate waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，伊莉絲再次看向一枚裂開的黑色封印。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。奧倫守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的黑門城等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The city celebrates its first repair festival rather than a military victory or a royal coronation.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 城市舉辦第一次修理節，而不是慶祝軍事勝利或君王加冕。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. A child asks why the festival honors people who once hated each other; Iris says they learned to share a burden.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 孩子問為何節日表揚過去互相憎恨的人，伊莉絲回答：因為他們學會共同承擔。


## CHAPTER 25｜No One Owns the Sky｜天空不屬於任何人

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：859**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Iris refused to begin with the official conclusion. Orren watches the ordinary blue sky above the repaired gate and chooses to live among the citizens who once recalled him. In the city of Blackgate, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。伊莉絲拒絕從官方結論開始。奧倫站在修好的城門下望著平凡的藍天，選擇與曾罷免他的居民一起生活。在黑門城，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Iris asked Orren to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。伊莉絲請奧倫說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The first spring after the peace charter arrived amid nervous anticipation. There was quiet anticipation before the first independent safety report was published. In the former military district, an apprentice engineer adjusted a heat valve while an experienced instructor checked the figures. The final report showed no evidence that anyone had tried to assassinate the city's executives. The old network had become a backbone of shared heating rather than a hidden weapon. Iris asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 和平憲章通過後的第一個春天，在緊張期待中到來。 第一份獨立安全報告公布前，現場有安靜的期待。 舊軍區裡，一名學徒工程師調整熱能閥門，資深教師核對數字。 最終報告沒有證據顯示有人試圖暗殺城市主管。 舊網路成了共同供暖的骨幹，不再是隱藏武器。 伊莉絲要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. Iris keeps the cracked black seal in a public museum, labeled not with a king’s name but with a warning about hidden obligations. Iris felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。伊莉絲把裂開的黑色封印存入公共博物館，標示的不是王名，而是對隱藏義務的警告。伊莉絲一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A public board could compute energy use and publish it where any resident might read. The court found no corpse connected with the alleged language fraud, despite rumors spread to inflame the public. A witness using a crutch was given a chair near the entrance rather than being forced to stand. The false theft charge had caused a porter's detention before the evidence was reviewed. Families could dwell in the repaired neighborhood without fearing an unannounced transfer. Orren marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 公共看板會計算能源使用量，張貼在任何居民都能閱讀的地方。 法庭確認語言造假指控與任何屍體無關，儘管有人散布流言煽動大眾。 一位拄拐杖的證人獲安排在入口附近坐下，沒有被迫站立。 虛假的失竊指控曾使搬運員在證據審閱前遭到拘留。 家庭可以住在修復後的社區，不必擔心未公告的轉權。 奧倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Hero Severin and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Iris rejected both. The issue was who carries the burden of keeping the world intact, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住英雄賽維林的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。伊莉絲兩者都拒絕。核心是誰有責任承擔維持世界完整的代價，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The office became frantic when several journalists demanded the verdict before it was issued. Children ran past a small hostel that now housed visitors from both territories. Halda was a knowledgeable navigator, yet she still invited younger keepers to challenge her choices. The judge noted that the altered clause had been dangerous but was not itself a lethal weapon. A lizard warmed itself on a stone wall, and for a moment the street seemed wonderfully ordinary. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 數名記者要求提前知道判決時，辦公室一片慌亂。 孩子們跑過一間小旅舍，如今裡面住著兩地旅人。 哈爾妲是知識豐富的引航者，仍邀年輕保管人質疑她的選擇。 法官指出遭改條款非常危險，卻不因此把它當作致命武器。 一隻蜥蜴在石牆上曬太陽，整條街忽然顯得平凡得美好。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The remainder of the restitution fund stayed in escrow for claims still under appeal. The judge's careful finding could stun the public without turning justice into entertainment. Successive inspections would continue after Lira resigned from the executive position. The treasury was ordered to return the diverted printing funds and publish a separate account of compensation. Orren recorded a slight vibration during the final demonstration and adjusted the support. A missing vowel in an old family name had once disguised the identity of a rightful lender. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 補償基金餘款留在受監督帳戶，供尚在申訴的案件使用。 法官謹慎的裁定可能震驚公眾，但不會把正義變成娛樂。 莉拉辭去執行長後，連續的檢查仍將繼續。 國庫被命令返還挪用的印製款，並另行公布補償帳目。 喬林在最後示範時記錄到輕微震動，於是調整支架。 舊家族姓氏少了一個母音，曾使真正出借者的身分被掩蓋。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Iris had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Orren wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Hero Severin. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。伊莉絲知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。奧倫把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起英雄賽維林注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

**EN** Iris did not call the day a perfect victory. Iris keeps the cracked black seal in a public museum, labeled not with a king’s name but with a warning about hidden obligations. What endured was a decision that the people of the city of Blackgate could challenge, revise, and make their own. Orren placed a broken black seal beside the public record. They left the last page open for names not yet written.

**繁中** 伊莉絲不把這一天稱為完美勝利。伊莉絲把裂開的黑色封印存入公共博物館，標示的不是王名，而是對隱藏義務的警告。真正留下的是黑門城人民有權質疑、修改並共同擁有的決定。奧倫把一枚裂開的黑色封印放在公共紀錄旁。他們把最後一頁留白，等待未來尚未寫上的姓名。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. Orren watches the ordinary blue sky above the repaired gate and chooses to live among the citizens who once recalled him.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 奧倫站在修好的城門下望著平凡的藍天，選擇與曾罷免他的居民一起生活。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Iris keeps the cracked black seal in a public museum, labeled not with a king’s name but with a warning about hidden obligations.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 伊莉絲把裂開的黑色封印存入公共博物館，標示的不是王名，而是對隱藏義務的警告。
